\|\tilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(s,t)}\|_{2}\leq\|\breve{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}
by Lemma
B.13
. Then by Theorem 5.3.4 in
15
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(s,t)}\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(s,t)}\circ\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(s,t)}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(s,t)}\|_{2}\max_{s\leq k\leq t}\bar{Q}^{(1,t)}_{k,k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(s\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

The following lemma provides upper bounds for several deterministic summations that will be used in the proofs throughout the subsequent sections.

Lemma B.15
.

Under Assumption
1
-
3
, for any
t\in[T]
and
k\in\{0,1\}
, we have

(1)

\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left|\Pi_{t,s}\right|\left|y_{s}(k)\right|\leq(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}R_{t}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}
.

(2)

\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left|\Pi_{t,s}\right|y_{s}^{2}(k)\leq c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}R_{t}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1/2}T^{1/2}
.

(3)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(k)\leq c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{3/2}R_{t}^{3}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/2}
.

(4)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-\nu_{1}}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}R_{r}^{-\nu_{2}}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(k)\leq c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\left(1-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/2\right)^{-1}T^{3/4-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/4}
for any
\nu_{1},\nu_{2}
such that
0\leq\nu_{1}
,
0\leq\nu_{2}\leq 1
and
\nu_{1}+\nu_{2}<2
. Suppose
T
is large enough.

(5)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{4}y_{r}^{4}(k)\leq c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{4}R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{3}T
.

(6)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\leq s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{1}}\right|^{3}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{2}}\right|\left|y_{r_{1}}(k)\right|^{3}\left|y_{r_{2}}(k)\right|\leq c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}\xi_{3}\xi_{8}^{1/4}R_{t}^{13/2}\eta_{t}^{7/4}T^{7/8}
.

(7)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}^{2}\Pi_{s,r_{2}}^{2}y_{r_{1}}^{2}(k)y_{r_{2}}^{2}(k)\leq c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}\xi_{3}R_{t}^{6}\eta_{t}^{2}T
.

(8)

\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\neq r_{3}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}^{2}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{2}}\right|\left|\Pi_{s,r_{3}}\right|y_{r_{1}}^{2}(k)\left|y_{r_{2}}(k)\right|\left|y_{r_{3}}(k)\right|\leq c_{1}^{4}\xi_{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}T
.

(9)

\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(k)\leq c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{2}^{2}R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{2}T
.

(10)

\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}\left(y_{r}(k)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{r},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{r}^{*}(k)\rangle\right)\right)^{2}R_{s}^{-1/2}y_{s}^{2}(k)\leq 2c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{5/4}T^{11/8}R_{t}^{5/2}\eta_{t}^{1/4}
for
T
large enough.

(11)

\sum_{1\leq t_{2},t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right|\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right|y^{2}_{t_{1}}(k)\left|y_{t_{2}}(k)\right|\left|y_{t_{3}}(k)\right|\left(\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{1}}^{2}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{1/4}\\
\leq 18c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{3/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)
for
T
large enough.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove the result for
k=1
.

(1)

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma
B.3
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left|\Pi_{t,s}\right|\left|y_{s}(1)\right|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}\right)~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R_{t}^{2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R_{t}^{2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.1}})\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

By Assumption
1
and similar method as in (1), we have

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left|\Pi_{t,s}\right|y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)\leq c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}T\kern 5.0pt.

(3)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
and the result in (2), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$ and Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{3}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-3/2}~~~~(\text{by the result in (2)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3/2}~~~~(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}R_{t}^{3}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{3/2}R_{t}^{3}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})\kern 5.0pt.

(4)

When
T
is large enough, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.10
and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-\nu_{1}}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}R_{r}^{-\nu_{2}}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{1-\nu_{1}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}R_{r}^{1-\nu_{2}}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{1-\nu_{1}+1-\nu_{2}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$ and $1-\nu_{2}\geq 0$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}+\eta_{s}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{r}\boldsymbol{x}_{r}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}+\eta_{s}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}+\eta_{s}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}+\eta_{s}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{s-1}+\eta_{s}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{s}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{2-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}+1}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-3/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2}\frac{T^{1-3/2+(3-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2})/4}}{1-(3/2-(3-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2})/2)}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.4}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}T^{1/2+1/4-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/4}\left(1-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/2\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\left(1-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/2\right)^{-1}T^{3/4-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/4}\kern 5.0pt.

(5)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{4}y_{r}^{4}(1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,r}^{4}\right)y_{r}^{4}(1)\quad(\text{rewrite the summation})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}R_{s}^{8}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-4}\right)y_{r}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$ and Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-4}\right)y_{r}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-4}\right)y_{r}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}R_{t}^{8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-4}\right)y_{t}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{4}R_{t}^{8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\eta_{t}^{3}y_{r}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{4}R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{3}T~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})\kern 5.0pt.

(6)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Hölder’s inequality and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\leq s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{1}}\right|^{3}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{2}}\right|\left|y_{r_{1}}(1)\right|^{3}\left|y_{r_{2}}(1)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|^{3}\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\right)\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|\left|y_{r}(1)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|^{3}\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\right)R_{s}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}\quad(\text{by the result in (1)})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|^{3}\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\right)R_{s}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1/2}(s-1)^{1/4}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/4}\quad(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}T^{1/4}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|^{3}\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\right)R_{s}\eta_{s}^{1/4}\quad(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{1/2}R_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|^{3}\right)\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\quad(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}R_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}R_{s}^{6}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-3}\right)\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\quad(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}R_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3}\right)\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}R_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3}\right)\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\quad(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}R_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}R_{t}^{6}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-3}\right)\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}\xi_{3}R_{t}^{13/2}T^{1/8}\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}((r-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-2}\left|y_{r}(1)\right|^{3}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}\xi_{3}R_{t}^{13/2}T^{1/8}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}((r-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-8}\right)^{1/4}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}y_{r}^{4}(1)\right)^{3/4}\quad(\text{H{\"{o}}lder's inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}\xi_{3}\xi_{8}^{1/4}R_{t}^{13/2}T^{1/8+3/4}\eta_{t}^{7/4}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3} and Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{7/2}\xi_{3}\xi_{8}^{1/4}R_{t}^{13/2}T^{7/8}\eta_{t}^{7/4}\kern 5.0pt.

(7)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}^{2}\Pi_{s,r_{2}}^{2}y_{r_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{r_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{4}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)^{2}\quad(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$ and Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{6}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-3}\quad(\text{by the result in (2)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3}\quad(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{t}^{6}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3}\xi_{3}R_{t}^{6}T\eta_{t}^{2}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})\kern 5.0pt.

(8)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Assumption
1
and the results in (1), (2), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}\neq r_{2}\neq r_{3}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}^{2}\left|\Pi_{s,r_{2}}\right|\left|\Pi_{s,r_{3}}\right|y_{r_{1}}^{2}(1)\left|y_{r_{2}}(1)\right|\left|y_{r_{3}}(1)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|\left|y_{r}(1)\right|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1+2}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\left|\Pi_{s,r}\right|\left|y_{r}(1)\right|\right)^{2}~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$, Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{4}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-5/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)~~~~(\text{results in (1) and (2)})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}T^{1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{4}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-5/2}(s-1)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{4}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-2}~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}~~~~(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}R_{t}^{4}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}\xi_{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{5/2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}T~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})\kern 5.0pt.

(9)

By Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}R_{r}^{4}((r-1)\vee\eta_{r}^{-1})^{-2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$ and Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}((r-1)R_{r}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}((r-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}R_{t}^{4}((r-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-2}\right)^{2}y_{s}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{2}^{2}R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{4}\xi_{2}^{2}R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{2}T~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})\kern 5.0pt.

(10)

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Corollary
B.4
, Lemma
B.9
, Lemma
B.11
and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}\left(y_{r}(1)-\langle x_{r},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{r}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\right)^{2}R_{s}^{-1/2}y_{s}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\left(y_{r}(1)-\langle x_{r},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{r}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right)R_{s}^{-1/2}y_{s}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\right)R_{s}^{-1/2}y_{s}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{coro4.3}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}R_{r}^{-1/2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)\quad(\text{change the summation order})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)c_{1}^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}R_{s}R_{r}^{1-1/2}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)c_{1}^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}R_{s}^{3/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}|\Pi_{s,r}|y_{r}^{2}(1)\right)~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{s}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)c_{1}^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}R_{s}^{3/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}y_{r}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)c_{1}^{4}T^{1+1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{3/2}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}\Pi_{s,s}^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}c_{1}^{4}T^{1+1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{3/2}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1}R_{s}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}c_{1}^{4}T^{3/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3/4}((s-1)\vee\eta_{s}^{-1})^{-1/4}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}c_{1}^{4}T^{3/2}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{s}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-3/4-1/2}\quad(\text{since $\eta_{s}\leq\eta_{1}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}c_{1}^{4}T^{3/2-1/8}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-5/4}~~~~(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}c_{1}^{4}T^{11/8}R_{t}^{5/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}((s-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-5/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{5/4}T^{11/8}R_{t}^{5/2}\eta_{t}^{1/4}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})\kern 5.0pt.

(11)

By AM-GM inequality and the definition of matrix
\boldsymbol{Q}^{(1,t)}
in Corollary
B.14
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{2},t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right|\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right|y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)\left|y_{t_{2}}(1)\right|\left|y_{t_{3}}(1)\right|\left(\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{1}}^{2}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{1\leq t_{2},t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\Bigg[\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{1}}^{2}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{2}}^{2}}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y^{2}_{t_{3}}(1)\left(\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{1}}^{2}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{3}}^{2}}\right)^{1/4}\Bigg]~~~~(\text{AM-GM inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{2}<t_{1}\leq t-1}t_{1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{1}}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{1\leq t_{1},t_{2}\leq t-1}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{1}}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{1\leq t_{1},t_{2}\leq t-1}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\kern 5.0pt.

Let
K=\lfloor\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\rfloor\in\mathbb{N}
. Since
\eta_{t}^{-1}=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{1/2}R_{t}^{2}\rparen=o(T)
by Assumption
3
,
K
tends to infinity when
T
grows. For
k=1,\ldots,K+1
, let
C_{k}=\lfloor\eta_{t}^{-1+(k-1)/K}T^{(k-1)/K}\rfloor
and let
C_{0}=0
. For
k=0,\ldots,K
, denote
B_{k}=\{C_{k}+1,\ldots,C_{k+1}\}\cap\{1,\ldots,t-1\}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{2}<t_{1},t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right|\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right|y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)\left|y_{t_{2}}(1)\right|\left|y_{t_{3}}(1)\right|\left(\frac{R_{t}^{2}}{R_{t_{1}}^{2}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\cdot\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{1\leq t_{1},t_{2}\leq t-1}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k_{1}=0}^{K}\sum_{k_{2}=0}^{K}\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{k_{1}}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{k_{2}}}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k_{1}=0}^{K}\sum_{k_{2}=0}^{K}S_{k_{1},k_{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

For
S_{0,0}
, by Corollary
B.14
, we have

\displaystyle S_{0,0}=

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{0}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{0}}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{0}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{0}}Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}^{(1,t)}\|_{2}\sum_{s\in B_{0}}y_{s}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}\cdot\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{t\in B_{0}}y_{t}^{4}(1)~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.5}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\sum_{s\in B_{0}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\kern 5.0pt.

For
k=1,\ldots,K
, by Corollary
B.14
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality on semi-definite positive matrix, we have

\displaystyle S_{k,0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{0}}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k+1}\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{k}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{0}}Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k+1}\left(\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in B_{k}}Q^{(t\land C_{k}+1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in B_{0}}Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k+1}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}^{(t\land C_{k}+1,t)}\right\|_{2}\sum_{s\in B_{k}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}^{(1,t)}\right\|_{2}\sum_{s\in B_{k}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k+1}\left(4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}C_{k}^{-1}\sum_{s\in B_{k}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}\sum_{s\in B_{0}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.5}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\left(\frac{C_{k+1}}{C_{k}}\frac{C_{k+1}}{\eta_{t}^{-1}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{0}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

For
k_{1},k_{2}=1,\ldots,K
, by Corollary
B.14
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality on semi-positive definite matrix, we have

\displaystyle S_{k_{1},k_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{k_{1}}}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{k_{2}}}(t_{1}\vee t_{2})Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}\left(\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in B_{k_{1}}}Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in B_{k_{2}}}Q^{(1,t)}_{t_{1},t_{2}}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}^{(t\land C_{k_{1}}+1,t)}\right\|_{2}\sum_{s\in B_{k_{1}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}^{(t\land C_{k_{2}}+1,t)}\right\|_{2}\sum_{s\in B_{k_{2}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}\left(4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}\xi_{2}R_{t}^{4}C_{k_{1}}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1}\in B_{k_{1}}}y_{t}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}C_{k_{2}}^{-1}\sum_{t_{2}\in B_{k_{2}}}y_{t}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.5}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\left(\frac{C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}}{C_{k_{1}}}\frac{C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}}{C_{k_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k_{1}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k_{2}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Since
K
tends to infinity when
T
grows to infinity, when
T
is large enough, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\frac{\eta_{t}^{-1+((k+1)-1)/K}T^{((k+1)-1)/K}}{\eta_{t}^{-1+(k-1)/K}T^{(k-1)/K}}=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\log(T\eta_{t})^{\frac{1}{K}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\frac{1}{K}\log(T\eta_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(\frac{2}{\log^{2}(T\eta_{t})}\log(T\eta_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 1\kern 5.0pt.

Since
C_{1}=\lfloor\eta_{t}^{-1}\rfloor\rightarrow\infty
, this implies that for
T
large enough, we have

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\frac{C_{k+1}}{C_{k}}\leq 2^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Then when
T
is large enough, by the construction method of
C_{1},\ldots,C_{K+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{k,k_{1},k_{2}=1,\ldots,K}\left\{1,\left(\frac{C_{k+1}}{C_{k}}\frac{C_{k+1}}{\eta_{t}^{-1}}\right)^{1/2},\left(\frac{C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}}{C_{k_{1}}}\frac{C_{k_{1}\vee k_{2}+1}}{C_{k_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2^{1/2}\cdot\frac{T}{\lfloor\eta_{t}^{-1}\rfloor}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{1/2}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}.

Hence by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Assumption
1
, for
T
large enough we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{2},t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right|\left|\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right|y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)\left|y_{t_{2}}(1)\right|\left|y_{t_{3}}(1)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k_{1}=0}^{K}\sum_{k_{2}=0}^{K}S_{k_{1},k_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{3/2}(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}\left[\sum_{k_{1}=0}^{K}\sum_{k_{2}=0}^{K}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k_{1}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k_{2}}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 18(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\sum_{s\in B_{k}}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 18(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}K\left(\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\sum_{C_{k}\leq t\leq C_{k+1}-1}y_{t}^{4}(1)\right)\right)~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 18(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}K\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}y_{t}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 18c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}(T\eta_{t})^{1/2}KT

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 18c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{3/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})\kern 5.0pt.

∎

The following lemma characterizes the relationship between the original
p
-space and the transformed
u
-space induced by the sigmoid function
\phi
.

Lemma B.16
.

Under Condition
1
, for any
u,\tilde{u}\geq 0
,
p=\phi(u)
and
\tilde{p}=\phi(\tilde{u})
, we have

(1)

\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}\right)^{\prime}\geq\frac{b_{3}}{2}
.

(2)

|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}|\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{1}{(1+u)^{2}}
.

(3)

\frac{b_{3}}{2}\cdot|u-\tilde{u}|\leq|\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}|\leq b_{1}\cdot|u-\tilde{u}|
.

(4)

|\frac{1}{p}-\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}|\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{|u-\tilde{u}|}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})}
.

(5)

|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}|\geq\frac{b_{3}}{2}\cdot\frac{|u-\tilde{u}|}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})(1+u\land\tilde{u})}
.

(6)

\max\left\{\frac{1}{\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}(u-\tilde{u}),\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}(u-\tilde{u})\right\}\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}-u}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})}
for
\tilde{u}\geq u
.

Proof.

(1)

By Condition
1
(1) and Condition
1
(3c), we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}\right)^{\prime}\geq\int_{-\infty}^{0}\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}u\geq b_{3}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{1}{(1-u)^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\geq\frac{b_{3}}{2}\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

By Condition
1
(1) and Condition
1
(3b), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\left|\int_{u}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{\phi(t)}\right)^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}t\right|\leq b_{2}\left|\int_{u}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{3}}\mathrm{d}t\right|\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2(1+u)^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

(3)

By Condition
1
(1) and Condition
1
(3a), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right|=\left|\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\bar{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right||u-\tilde{u}|=\left|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(-\bar{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right||u-\tilde{u}|\leq b_{1}|u-\tilde{u}|\kern 5.0pt.

By the result in (1) and Condition
1
(2), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\tilde{u}}^{u}\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(t)}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{d}t\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\tilde{u}}^{u}\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{d}t\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}}{2}|u-\tilde{u}|\kern 5.0pt.

(4)

By the result in (2), we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p}-\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right|\leq\left|\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\left(\frac{1}{\phi(t)}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{d}t\right|\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2}\left|\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{2}}\mathrm{d}t\right|=\frac{b_{2}|u-\tilde{u}|}{2(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})}\kern 5.0pt.

(5)

By Condition
1
(3c), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right|\geq

\displaystyle b_{3}\left|\int_{\tilde{u}}^{u}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{3}}\mathrm{d}t\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}}{2}\left|\frac{1}{(1+\tilde{u})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(1+u)^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}}{2}\frac{(1+\tilde{u}+u)}{(1+\tilde{u})^{2}(1+u)^{2}}|u-\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}}{2}\frac{1}{(1+\tilde{u})(1+u)(1+\tilde{u}\land u)}|u-\tilde{u}|\kern 5.0pt.

(6)

By Condition
1
(1), Condition
1
(2) and the result in (4), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}(u-\tilde{u})\leq\frac{1}{\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\leq\frac{b_{2}(\tilde{u}-u)}{2(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})}\kern 5.0pt.

By Condition
1
(2) and Condition
1
(3b), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}-\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}(u-\tilde{u})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\int_{t}^{\tilde{u}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(s)}\right)^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\int_{t}^{\tilde{u}}\left(\frac{1}{\phi(-s)}\right)^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{2}\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\int_{t}^{\tilde{u}}\frac{1}{(1+s)^{3}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}}{2}\int_{u}^{\tilde{u}}\left[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{2}}-\frac{1}{(1+\tilde{u})^{2}}\right]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}}{2}\left[\frac{1}{1+u}-\frac{1}{1+\tilde{u}}-\frac{1}{(1+\tilde{u})^{2}}(\tilde{u}-u)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{(1+\tilde{u})(\tilde{u}-u)-(1+u)(\tilde{u}-u)}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{(\tilde{u}-u)^{2}}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}-u}{(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

B.3
Regret Decomposition

In this subsection, we derive a decomposition of the Neyman regret into two components: the probability regret and the prediction regret. We start by deriving the oracle variance in the following proposition.

Proposition
3.1
.

The oracle variance is given by
T\cdot\textrm{V}^{*}=2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)

and the Neyman allocation is given by the least squares predictors
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(k)=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{\textrm{OLS}}(k)
and assignment probability
p=(1+\mathcal{E}(0)/\mathcal{E}(1))^{-1}
.

Proof.

By direct calculation,
\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p)
is given by:

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p)

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\rangle\big\}^{2}\right)\cdot\Big\lparen\frac{1}{p}-1\Big\rparen+\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle\big\}^{2}\right)\cdot\Big\lparen\frac{1}{1-p}-1\Big\rparen

\displaystyle+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\rangle\big\}\cdot\big\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle\big\}\kern 5.0pt.

By fixing
\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)
, it is easy to see that the optimal choice of
p
is
p^{*}=(1+(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle)^{2})^{1/2}/(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\rangle)^{2})^{1/2})^{-1}
. By plugging the form of
p^{*}
into the variance, we can obtain

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p^{*})=

\displaystyle 2\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\rangle\big\}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle\big\}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\rangle\big\}\cdot\big\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle\big\}\kern 5.0pt.

Now we prove that
\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p^{*})\geq\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0),p^{*})
for any
\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)
.
Let
\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}
.
We further denote
\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}=\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)
,
\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}=\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)
,
\boldsymbol{z}_{1}=\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{z}_{0}=\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}
.
By the explicit form of
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)
, we can easily prove that
\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\rangle=\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle=\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0},\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\rangle=\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle=0
.
Hence we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p^{*})=

\displaystyle 2\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}-\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}-\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}-\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}-\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\rangle+2\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0),p^{*})=

\displaystyle 2\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\rangle\kern 5.0pt.

Hence it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}+\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle\geq\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Note that we have
\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle\geq-\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}
by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.
Then we only need to show that

\displaystyle\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\geq\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}\kern 5.0pt.

(12)

Since both sides of (
12
) are nonnegative, it is equivalent to

\displaystyle\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\geq\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+2(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2})^{1/2}+\left(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\kern 5.0pt,

which can be further simplified as

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 2(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|_{2}^{2})^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

This is immediately verified by AM-GM inequality.
Hence
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)
minimize
\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau};\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0),p^{*})
.
By plugging in
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)
, we can derive the minimizer for
p
as
p^{*}=(1+\mathcal{E}(0)/\mathcal{E}(1))^{-1}
.
∎

Based on Proposition
3.1
, we can derive the decomposition of the Neyman regret as stated in the following lemma.

Lemma
3.3
.

Under Assumption
1
, the Neyman Regret can be decomposed as the
T
-normalized sum of the probability regret and the prediction regret:

\mathcal{R}^{\textrm{Neyman}}_{T}=\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{pred}}_{T}]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Let us recall the expression for the variance of the AIPW estimator from Proposition
2.2
:

\displaystyle T\cdot\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-1\right)\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(p_{t})\right]+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(p^{*})\right]+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+S_{1}\kern 5.0pt.

By the definition of prediction regret, we can further decompose term
S_{1}
as:

\displaystyle S_{1}=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p^{*}}-1\right)+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p^{*}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{p^{*}}-1\right)\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}-1\right)\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}+\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}+2\rho\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right\}\cdot\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

Hence the Neyman regret can be finally decomposed by:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\textrm{Neyman}}_{T}=

\displaystyle T\cdot\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen-T\cdot\textrm{V}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}\right]-2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

∎

B.4
Probability Regret

In this subsection, we aim to analyze the inverse weighting terms that are appearing in the procedure and derive an upper bound for the probability regret.
Throughout the analysis, we find it important to control the expectation of the inverse probability.
This bound is established through a sequence of intermediate results, including Lemma
4.14
, Corollary
4.15
, Lemma
4.16
, and Corollary
B.17
.

In particular, Lemma
4.14
provides an upper bound for the inverse probability under the given optimization program.

Lemma
4.14
.

Consider
A,B\geq 0
an define
p^{*}
as the minimizer of the following program:

\displaystyle p^{*}=\argmin_{p\in(0,1)}\frac{A}{p}+\frac{B}{1-p}+\eta^{-1}\Psi(p)\kern 5.0pt.

Under Condition
1
, the minimizer
p^{*}
is bounded away from
0
and
1
in the following sense:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{*}}\leq

\displaystyle 2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta^{1/4}B^{1/4}\land b_{1}(b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(B/A)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}\leq

\displaystyle 2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta^{1/4}A^{1/4}\land b_{1}(b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(A/B)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

If
A\geq B
, then we have
p^{*}\geq 1/2
and
1/p^{*}\leq 2
. By definition,
u^{*}=\phi(p^{*})\geq 0
should satisfy the following first-order equation:

\displaystyle A\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}+B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}+\eta^{-1}(u^{*}+3(u^{*})^{2})=0\kern 5.0pt.

By Lemma
B.16
and Condition
1
, we have

\displaystyle 3\eta^{-1}(u^{*})^{2}\leq\eta^{-1}(u^{*}+3(u^{*})^{2})+B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}=-A\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}\leq\frac{b_{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{A}{(1+u^{*})^{2}}\leq\frac{b_{2}A}{2(u^{*})^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we have
u^{*}\leq(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta^{1/4}A^{1/4}
. Moreover, by Lemma
B.16
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}B}{2}\leq B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}\right)^{\prime}\leq B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}\leq\eta^{-1}(u^{*}+3(u^{*})^{3})+B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u^{*})}\right)^{\prime}\leq\frac{b_{2}A}{2(1+u^{*})^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

This implies that
u^{*}\leq(b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(A/B)^{1/2}-1
. Then by Condition
1
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}=\frac{1}{1-\phi(u^{*})}\leq\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}+b_{1}u^{*}\leq 2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta^{1/4}A^{1/4}\land b_{1}(b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(A/B)^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

We can derive similar results when
A<B
, i.e.,
\frac{1}{p^{*}}\leq 2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta^{1/4}B^{1/4}\land b_{1}(b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(B/A)^{1/2}
.
∎

Based Lemma
4.14
and Jensen’s inequality, we can upper bound the moments of the inverse probabilities in terms of the expectation of the estimated squared residuals.
The result is summarized in the following corollary.

Corollary
4.15
.

Under Condition
1
, for each iteration
t\in[T]
and any
0\leq k\leq 4
, the
k
th moment of the inverse probabilities are bounded as

\displaystyle\Esym\Big[\Big\lparen\frac{1}{p_{t}}\Big\rparen^{k}\Big]\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t-1}(0)\right]^{1/4}\right)^{k},

\displaystyle\Esym\Big[\Big\lparen\frac{1}{1-p_{t}}\Big\rparen^{k}\Big]\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t-1}(1)\right]^{1/4}\right)^{k}.

Proof.

By the definition of
p_{t}
, Lemma
4.14
and Jensen’s inequality, for any
0\leq k\leq 4
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{t}^{k}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{t-1}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{k}\right]\leq\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t-1}(0)\right]^{1/4}\right)^{k}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we can prove the result for the moments of
1/(1-p_{t})
.
∎

Corollary
4.15
implies that we need to further bound the expectation of the estimated squared residuals, which is explicitly derived in the following lemma.

Lemma
4.16
.

For any
t\in[T]
, the expectation of the estimated squared residuals is equal to

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]

\displaystyle=A^{*}_{t}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\Esym\Big[\frac{1}{p_{r}}-1\Big]

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(0)]

\displaystyle=A^{*}_{t}(0)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(0)\Esym\Big[\frac{1}{1-p_{r}}-1\Big]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
4.12
and the law of iterated expectation, the expectation of
\widehat{A}_{t}(1)
is calculated by:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}\cdot\left(y_{s}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}\cdot\left(y_{s}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(y_{s}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathrm{E}\left[y_{s}^{2}(1)-2y_{s}(1)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle+\sum_{1\leq r_{1},r_{2}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}\Pi_{s,r_{2}}y_{r_{1}}(1)y_{r_{2}}(1)\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r_{1}}=1]}{p_{r_{1}}}\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r_{2}}=1]}{p_{r_{2}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{s}^{2}(1)-2y_{s}(1)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}^{*}(1)\rangle+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r}=1]}{p_{r}}\right)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{r-1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq s-1}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[\Pi_{s,r_{1}}\Pi_{s,r_{2}}y_{r_{1}}(1)y_{r_{2}}(1)\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r_{1}}=1]}{p_{r_{1}}}\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r_{2}}=1]}{p_{r_{2}}}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{r_{2}-1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{s}^{2}(1)-2y_{s}(1)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}^{*}(1)\rangle+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{r}}\right]+2\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{1\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq s-1}\Pi_{s,r_{1}}\Pi_{s,r_{2}}y_{r_{1}}(1)y_{r_{2}}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(y_{s}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{r}}-1\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{t}^{*}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{r}}-1\right]\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we can prove that

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(0)]=A_{t}^{*}(0)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(0)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{1-p_{r}}-1\right]\kern 5.0pt.

∎

The following corollary characterized the rate of the expectation of the estimated squared residuals.

Corollary B.17
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for any
t\in[T]
, the expectation of the estimated squared residuals can be bounded as:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)],\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(0)]\right\}\leq\kappa=(2c_{1}^{2}+o(1))T\kern 5.0pt,

where
\kappa>2c_{1}^{2}T
is the largest solution to the following equation:

\displaystyle\kappa=2c_{1}^{2}T+c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{3/2}\left(b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}+1\right)R_{T}^{2}T^{1/4}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We use induction method to prove the result.
For
t=1
, we have
\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}(1)=A_{t}^{*}(1)=y_{t}(1)^{2}\leq c_{1}^{2}T^{1/2}\leq 2c_{1}^{2}T\leq\kappa
and
\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}(0)=A_{t}^{*}(0)=y_{t}(0)^{2}\leq c_{1}^{2}T^{1/2}\leq 2c_{1}^{2}T\leq\kappa
.
Hence the result holds.
If the result is proved for
1,\ldots,t-1
, then by Lemma
B.11
, Lemma
B.15
, Lemma
4.16
and Corollary
4.15
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]=

\displaystyle A_{t}^{*}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{r}}-1\right]~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:expectation-of-estores}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)\left(1+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{r}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{r-1}(0)\right]^{1/4}\right)~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment}, Lemma \ref{coro4.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}\eta_{r}^{1/4}y_{r}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{induction assumption})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1/2}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+\left(b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}+1\right)\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}y_{r}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{3/2}\left(b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}+1\right)R_{t}^{3}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.9}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{1}^{2}T+c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{3/2}\xi_{3/2}\left(b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}+1\right)R_{T}^{2}T^{1/4}\quad(\text{since $R_{t}\leq R_{T}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\kappa\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we can prove that
\mathrm{E}[\widehat{A}_{t}(0)]\leq\kappa
.
The result is thus proved by induction method.
By Assumption
3
, we have
R_{T}^{2}T^{1/4}=o(T^{3/4})
.
Hence it is easy to see that
\kappa=(2c_{1}^{2}+o(1))T
.
∎

Under Corollary
4.15
and Corollary
B.17
, we are now able to control the expectations of powers of inverse probabilities.
The following lemma extends these results by providing upper bounds for moments of the inverse weighting terms that arise in the subsequent proofs, beyond the simple power-type functions.

Lemma B.18
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

(1)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{4}\right]=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{3/8}\rparen
.

(2)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{5/16}\rparen
.

(3)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{9/32}\rparen
.

(4)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{3}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{25/128}\rparen
.

(5)

For
s_{3}\neq s_{2}<s_{1}
,
\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right)\right|\\
\leq 4\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right]^{1/4}\right)^{25/16}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{2}}}\right)^{15/96}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{3}}}\right)^{15/96}
.

(6)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{4}\right]=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{3/8}\rparen
.

(7)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{5/16}\rparen
.

(8)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{9/32}\rparen
.

(9)

\max_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{3}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{25/128}\rparen
.

(10)

For
s_{3}\neq s_{2}<s_{1}
,
\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=0]}{1-p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right)\right|\\
\leq 4\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\left[\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(1)\right]^{1/4}\right)^{25/16}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{2}}}\right)^{15/96}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{3}}}\right)^{15/96}
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove (1)-(5).

(1)

For any
s_{1}\in[T]
, by Corollary
4.15
and Corollary
B.17
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{4}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}\right)^{4}\right]+1

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s}^{3}}\right]+1

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{3}+1

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}T^{-1/8}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{3}+1

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{3/8}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

If
s_{1}<s_{2}
, then by law of iterated expectation, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\right]=0\kern 5.0pt.

If
s_{2}<s_{1}
, then by law of iterated expectation, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{3}}+1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}+1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{3}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{3}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(2+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{2}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(2+\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2}}\right]\right)^{1/2}+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(2+\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]\right)^{1/2}+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(2+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{4\cdot\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}}+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{5/16}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

(3)

For any
1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T
, by law of iterated expectation, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{2}}+1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2}}+1\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{2}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}^{2}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2}}\right]+1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{2\cdot\frac{4}{3}}}\right]\right)^{3/4}+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{5/3}}\right]\right)^{3/4}+2\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)+1\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{4\cdot\frac{1}{4}+\frac{5}{3}\cdot\frac{3}{4}}+2\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)+1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{9/32}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

(4)

If
s_{2}
or
s_{3}
is the largest number among
\{s_{1},s_{2},s_{3}\}
, then by law of iterated expectation, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]=0\kern 5.0pt.

If
s_{1}
is the largest number among
\{s_{1},s_{2},s_{3}\}
, we assume WLOG that
s_{3}<s_{2}
. By law of iterated expectation, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the result in (3), we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1-p_{s_{1}}}{p_{s_{1}}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}+1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}+1\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}+S_{4}\kern 5.0pt.

By Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
, we can bound
S_{1}
as:

\displaystyle S_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)\kern 5.0pt.

By law of iterated expectation and Hölder’s inequality, we can bound
S_{2}
as:

\displaystyle S_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{4/3}}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{1/3}}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{4\cdot\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{3}\cdot\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{5/4}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly,
S_{3}
can be bounded as:

\displaystyle S_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{5/4}\kern 5.0pt.

By Hölder’s inequality, we can bound
S_{4}
as:

\displaystyle S_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{4/3}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{4/3}}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{1/3}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{4/3}}\right]\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{12}}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{\frac{4}{3}\cdot\frac{12}{11}}}\right]\right)^{\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{11}{12}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{4}}\right]\right)^{\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{12}}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{3}}^{\frac{5}{11}}}\right]\right)^{\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{11}{12}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{4\cdot\frac{1}{4}+4\cdot\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{12}+\frac{5}{11}\cdot\frac{3}{4}\cdot\frac{11}{12}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{1}^{1/4}\kappa^{1/4}\right)^{25/16}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{25/128}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

(5)

For any
1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T
, by law of total variance we have

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathrm{E}\Bigg[\operatorname{Cov}\Bigg(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s_{1}-1}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}\Bigg[\mathrm{E}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s_{1}-1}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s_{1}-1}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1-p_{s_{1}}}{p_{s_{1}}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}\right]\right)\kern 5.0pt.

By similar method as in (4), we can show that

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{2}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{3}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{2}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1/4}\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{3}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{15/48}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)

\displaystyle+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{2}}}\right)^{1/8}

\displaystyle+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{3}}}\right)^{1/8}

\displaystyle+\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{1+1/4+15/48}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{2}}}\right)^{1/8}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{3}}}\right)^{15/96}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{s_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{s_{1}}(0)\right)^{25/16}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{2}}}\right)^{15/96}\left(\frac{R_{s_{1}}}{R_{s_{3}}}\right)^{15/96}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

With the help of Lemma
B.18
, we can establish an upper bound for the fourth moment of the random online OLS residuals, similar to the bound for the deterministic online OLS residuals given in Lemma
B.7
except for an additional smaller order term. We first given the upper bound on the tracking error term in the following proposition.

Proposition
4.17
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, the prediction tracking error for for both treatments
k\in\{0,1\}
can be bounded as
