\Esym[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(k)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(k)\rangle^{4}]=o\lparen T^{1/2}R^{2}\rparen
.

Proof.

By explicit calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\eta_{t}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}y_{s}(1)\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}-1\right]\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{4}y_{s}^{4}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}-1\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle+T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq t-1}|\Pi_{t,s_{1}}|^{3}|\Pi_{t,s_{2}}||y_{s_{1}}(1)|^{3}|y_{s_{2}}(1)|\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{3}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\leq t-1}\Pi_{t,s_{1}}^{2}\Pi_{t,s_{2}}^{2}y_{s_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{s_{2}}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-1}\sum_{1\leq s_{1}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{3}\leq t-1}\Pi_{t,s_{1}}^{2}|\Pi_{t,s_{2}}||\Pi_{t,s_{3}}|y_{s_{1}}^{2}(1)|y_{s_{2}}(1)||y_{s_{3}}(1)|

\displaystyle\times\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}S_{1}+T^{-1/2}S_{2}+T^{-1/2}S_{3}+T^{-1/2}S_{4}\kern 5.0pt.

By Assumption
3
, Lemma
B.15
and Lemma
B.18
, we have

\displaystyle S_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{T}^{8}\eta_{T}^{3}T\cdot T^{3/8}=TR_{T}^{2}\cdot T^{-9/8}=o(TR_{T}^{2})\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle S_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{t}^{13/2}\eta_{t}^{7/4}T^{7/8}\cdot T^{5/16}=TR_{T}^{2}\cdot(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-11/16}\cdot R_{T}^{-7/4}=o(TR_{T}^{2})\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle S_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{T}^{6}\eta_{T}^{2}T\cdot T^{9/32}=TR_{T}^{2}\cdot(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-23/32}\cdot R_{T}^{-23/8}=o(TR_{T}^{2})\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle S_{4}\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{T}^{4}\eta_{T}T\cdot T^{25/128}=TR_{T}^{2}\cdot(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-39/128}\cdot R_{T}^{-39/32}=o(TR_{T}^{2})\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Lemma
B.5
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\leq T^{-1/2}(S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}+S_{4})=o(T^{1/2}R_{T}^{2})=o(T^{1/2}R^{2})\kern 5.0pt.

∎

The following corollary is a direct consequence of the proof in Proposition
4.17
and will be used in the proofs of Lemma
C.3
and Lemma
C.18
.

Corollary B.19
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for
k\in\{0,1\}
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{89/128}R_{t}^{2}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By similar proof as in Proposition
4.17
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{t}R_{s}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{s}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{3}T\cdot T^{3/8}+R_{t}^{13/2}\eta_{t}^{7/4}T^{7/8}\cdot T^{5/16}+R_{t}^{6}\eta_{t}^{2}T\cdot T^{9/32}+R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}T\cdot T^{25/128}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{89/128}R_{t}^{2}\left(T^{-105/128}+R_{t}T^{-49/128}+T^{-53/128}+1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{89/128}R_{t}^{2}\left(T^{-105/128}+(TR_{t}^{-4})^{-49/128}R_{t}^{-17/32}+T^{-53/128}+1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{89/128}R_{t}^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

By combining the results in Lemma
B.7
and Proposition
4.17
, we can obtain the upper bound on the fourth moment of the random online OLS residuals in the following lemma.

Lemma
4.6
*
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for each treatment
k\in\{0,1\}
, the fourth moment of the online residuals is bounded as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(k)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(k)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\left[c_{1}^{4}\max\{c_{2},1\}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}+o(1)\right]T^{1/2}R^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove the result for
k=1
.
By Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]+4\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{3}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+6\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{3}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)^{3/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+6\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\right)^{3/4}+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)^{1/4}+\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\right)^{1/4}\right]^{4}\kern 5.0pt.

(13)

Hence by Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
B.7
and Proposition
4.17
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}R_{T}^{2}T^{1/2}+o(R^{2}T^{1/2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[c_{1}^{4}\max\{c_{2},1\}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}+o(1)\right]T^{1/2}R^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

We now turn to the analysis of the probability regret. The following lemma is a direct implication of the adaptive sequential design.

Lemma
4.2
.

The estimated sigmoid loss functions are conditionally unbiased:
\Esym[\widehat{h}_{t}(u)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=h_{t}(u)
for all
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
-measurable random variables
u
.

Proof.

Since
\beta_{t}
,
p_{t}
and
u
are measurable with respect to
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{h}_{t}(u)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\Big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\Big\rparen^{2}\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot\frac{1}{\phi(u)}+\Big\lparen y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\Big\rparen^{2}\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\cdot\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\phi(u)}+(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{t}(u)\kern 5.0pt.

∎

In Lemma
4.3
and Lemma
B.20
, we establish the lower and upper bounds of the utilized Bregman divergence, respectively.

Lemma
4.3
.

The Bregman divergence is bounded:

\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(v|u)\geq\frac{1}{2}(v-u)^{2}(1+\frac{1}{2}|v|+|u|)
.

Proof.

Recall that
\psi(u)=\frac{1}{2}u^{2}+|u|^{3}
. Using the shorthand
h(u)=\frac{1}{2}u^{2}
and
g(u)=|u|^{3}
, we have that
\psi(u)=h(u)+g(u)
. Using the properties of Bregman divergence, we can separate the Bregman divergence as

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(v|u)=\mathcal{B}_{h+g}(v|u)=\mathcal{B}_{h}(v|u)+\mathcal{B}_{g}(v|u)=\frac{1}{2}(v-u)^{2}+\mathcal{B}_{g}(v|u)\kern 5.0pt.

To complete the proof, it suffices to lower bound the second Bregman divergence as

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{g}(v|u)\geq\frac{1}{4}(v-u)^{2}\cdot(|v|+2|u|)\kern 5.0pt.

(14)

To this end, we derive the Bregman divergence corresponding to
g
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{g}(v|u)=|v|^{3}-|u|^{3}-(3|u|u)\cdot(v-u)=|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}-3uv|u|\kern 5.0pt.

To show the inequality (
14
) holds, we will consider two cases.

Case 1.
In the first case, we will suppose that
u
and
v
have the same sign, i.e.,
uv\geq 0
.
In this case, it is true that
|u-v|=|u|+|v|
. Thus, we have that the Bregman divergence is given by

B_{g}(v|u)=|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}-3|u|^{2}|v|\,\kern 5.0pt.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle(v-u)^{2}(|v|+2|u|)=

\displaystyle\left(v^{2}+u^{2}-2vu\right)(|v|+2|u|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|v|^{3}+2v^{2}|u|+u^{2}|v|+2\left|u\right|^{3}-2vu(|v|+2|u|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|v|^{3}+2v^{2}|u|+u^{2}|v|+2\left|u\right|^{3}-2|v||u|(|v|+2|u|)\quad(\text{because }uv>0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}-3u^{2}|v|\kern 5.0pt.

Thus, this establishes that inequality (
14
) holds in this case, as we have

B_{g}(v|u)=(v-u)^{2}(|v|+2|u|)\geq\frac{1}{4}(v-u)^{2}(|v|+2|u|)\kern 5.0pt.

Case 2.
In the second case, we will suppose that
v
and
u
have different signs, i.e.,
vu<0
.
This means that
vu=-|v||u|
. Before continuing, we establish a handy upper bound, which is that

4v^{2}|u|\leq 2\big(|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+|u|^{2}v\big)\kern 5.0pt.

(15)

This upper bound may be established using the AM-GM inequality, as

4v^{2}|u|=4|v|^{3/2}\cdot(|v|^{1/2}|u|)\leq 2\big(|v|^{3}+|v||u|^{2}\big)\leq 2\big(|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+|v|u^{2}\big)\kern 5.0pt.

With that inequality established, let us turn our attention back to the Bregman divergence.
In this case, the Bregman divergence is

\displaystyle B_{g}(v|u)

\displaystyle=|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}-3uv|u|

\displaystyle=|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+3|u||v||u|\qquad\text{(because $uv<0$)}

\displaystyle=|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+3u^{2}|v|\kern 5.0pt.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}(v-u)^{2}(|v|+2|u|)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big(v^{2}+u^{2}-2vu\big)(|v|+2|u|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}(v^{2}+u^{2}+2|v||u|)(|v|+2|u|)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\{|v|^{3}+2v^{2}|u|+2|u|^{3}+u^{2}|v|+2v^{2}|u|+4|v|u^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\{|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+4v^{2}|u|+5|v|u^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\{|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+2\left(|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+|v|u^{2}\right)+6|v|u^{2}\right\}\quad(\text{use \eqref{eq:upperbound}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\{3|v|^{3}+5|u|^{3}+7|v|u^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|v|^{3}+2|u|^{3}+3|v|u^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{g}(v|u)\kern 5.0pt.

Thus, we have established that the inequality (
14
) holds for all
v,u\in\mathbb{R}
, which completes the proof.
∎

The upper bound of the probability is derived through standard FTRL analysis.
We first state one key property of the Bregman divergence.

Lemma B.20
.

Suppose that
f
and
\psi
are both differentiable and
f-\psi
is convex. Then, for all
x,y\in\mathcal{X}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{f}(x|y)\leq\sup_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\left\{\left\langle\nabla f(y)-\nabla f(x),z-x\right\rangle-\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(z|x)\right\}.

Proof.

We begin the proof by showing that the the following inequality is true:

\displaystyle f(z)-f(x)-\langle\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle\geq\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)\quad\text{for all }z\in\mathcal{X}\kern 5.0pt.

(16)

To this end,

\displaystyle f(z)-f(x)-\langle\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big\{f(z)-f(x)-\langle\nabla f(x),z-x\rangle-B_{\psi}(z|x)\Big\}

(add + subtract)

\displaystyle=\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)+\Big\{B_{f}(z|x)-B_{\psi}(z|x)\Big\}

(definition of
B_{f}
)

\displaystyle=\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)+B_{f-\psi}(z|x)

(linearity of Bregman divergence)

\displaystyle\geq\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)

\displaystyle\text{($f-\psi$ is convex)}\kern 5.0pt.

We are now ready to proceed with the proof of the main lemma.
Define the auxiliary function
\phi_{y}:\mathcal{X}\to\mathbb{R}
as

\phi_{y}(z)=f(z)-\langle\nabla f(y),z\rangle.

Observe that
\phi_{y}
is convex and differentiable. Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\phi_{y}(y)

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\phi_{y}(z)

\displaystyle=\phi_{y}(x)+\inf_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\{\phi_{y}(z)-\phi_{y}(x)\}

\displaystyle=\phi_{y}(x)+\inf_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\Big\{f(z)-f(x)-\langle\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle\Big\}

(definition of
\phi_{y}
)

\displaystyle\geq\phi_{y}(x)+\inf_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\Big\{\langle\nabla f(x)-\nabla f(y),z-x\rangle+B_{\psi}(z|x)\Big\}

\displaystyle\text{(Using \eqref{lem4.19_eq1})}\kern 5.0pt.

Rearranging terms and using the definition of
\phi_{y}(x)
and
B_{f}
, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in\mathcal{X}}\Big\{\langle\nabla f(y)-\nabla f(x),z-x\rangle-B_{\psi}(z|x)\Big\}

\displaystyle\geq\phi_{y}(x)-\phi_{y}(y)

\displaystyle=f(x)-f(y)-\langle\nabla f(y),x-y\rangle

\displaystyle=B_{f}(x|y)\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Based on Lemma
4.3
, Lemma
B.20
and the sigmoid transformation, we can derive the following upper bound for the probability regret.

Proposition
4.4
.

The expected probability regret can be bounded as

\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{T}}\psi(u^{*})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\Esym\Bigg[\frac{\big\lparen\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})\big\rparen^{2}}{2(1+|u_{t}|)}\Bigg]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We introduce the following notations:

\displaystyle\widehat{H}_{t}(u)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{h}_{s}(u)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t}}\psi(u)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{t}(u)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{h}_{s}(u)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t-1}}\psi(u)\kern 5.0pt.

Let
u_{t}
,
\tilde{u}_{t}
be the minimizer of functions
\widehat{H}_{t}
and
\widetilde{H}_{t}
, respectively. By Lemma
4.2
, law of iterated expectation and similar proof as in of Lemma
4.9
, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[f_{t}(p_{t})-f_{t}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[h_{t}(u_{t})-h_{t}(u^{*})\right]\quad(\text{sigmoid transformation})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})-\widehat{h}_{t}(u^{*})\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right]\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:unbiased-sigmoid-loss}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})-\widehat{h}_{t}(u^{*})\right]\quad(\text{law of iterated expectation})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}\psi(u^{*})+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(u_{t})-\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right)\right]\quad(\text{similar proof as Lemma \ref{lemma:standard-oco-for-pred-regret}})\kern 5.0pt.

(17)

Then by Lemma
B.20
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(u_{t})-\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\langle\nabla\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1}),u_{t}-\tilde{u}_{t+1}\rangle+\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]\quad(\text{optimality of $\tilde{u}_{t+1}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\left\{\langle\nabla\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})-\nabla\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(u_{t}),z-u_{t}\rangle-\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(z|u_{t})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\left\{\left\langle\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}),u_{t}-z\right\rangle-\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(z|u_{t})\right\}\right]\quad(\text{optimality of $\tilde{u}_{t+1}$ and $u_{t}$})\kern 5.0pt.

(18)

For any
t\in[T]
and any fixed
z\in\mathbb{R}
, by Lemma
4.3
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}),u_{t}-z\right\rangle-\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(z|u_{t})\leq

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}),u_{t}-z\right\rangle-\frac{\eta_{t}^{-1}}{2}(z-u_{t})^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{2}|z|+|u_{t}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}),u_{t}-z\right\rangle-\frac{\eta_{t}^{-1}}{2}(z-u_{t})^{2}\left(1+|u_{t}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{t}[\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})]^{2}}{2(1+|u_{t}|)}\kern 5.0pt.

(19)

By (
B.4
), (
B.4
) and (
B.4
), we can derive

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{T}}\psi(u^{*})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{(\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}))^{2}}{2(1+|u_{t}|)}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Given the upper bound of the probability regret established in Proposition
4.4
, we further upper bound the two corresponding terms in Lemma
4.5
and Lemma
B.21
, respectively.

Lemma
4.5
.

Under Condition
1
,
the conditional expectation of the squared gradient term is at most

\Esym\Bigg[\frac{\big\lparen\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})\big\rparen^{2}}{1+|u_{t}|}~\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Bigg]\leq b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}\Big\lparen\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\}^{4}+\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\}^{4}\Big\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Thus, applying the law of iterated expectation yields

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\Esym\Bigg[\frac{\big\lparen\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t})\big\rparen^{2}}{2(1+|u_{t}|)}\Bigg]\leq\frac{1}{2}b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}\sum_{k\in\{0,1\}}\Esym\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\big\{y_{t}(k)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(k)\rangle\big\}^{4}\Bigg]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

The gradient of the estimated loss function can be computed as

\displaystyle\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}\left(u\right)=

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}\kern 5.0pt.

Using Assumption
1
, Condition
1
and the fact that the cross term is zero, we can upper bound the square of the gradient at
u_{t}
as

\displaystyle(\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}\left(u_{t}\right))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}^{2}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\left[\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u_{t})}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{2}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{(1-p_{t})^{2}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u_{t})}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{1}^{2}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{\phi(u_{t})}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{1-\phi(u_{t})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{1}^{2}\Big[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\phi(0)}+b_{1}|u_{t}|\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(0)}+b_{1}|u_{t}|\right)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{1}^{2}(2+b_{1}|u_{t}|)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{(\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}\left(u_{t}\right))^{2}}{1+|u_{t}|}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{b_{1}^{2}(2+b_{1}|u_{t}|)}{1+|u_{t}|}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}\left[\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Lemma B.21
.

Under Assumption
1
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\psi(u^{*})\leq\frac{8}{b_{3}^{3}}\left[\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{3}+\frac{b_{3}}{4}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{2}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Since
p^{*}=(1+\mathcal{E}(0)/\mathcal{E}(1))^{-1}
, by Assumption
1
we have
p^{*}\in[\frac{1}{1+c_{1}/c_{0}},1-\frac{1}{1+c_{1}/c_{0}}]
.
For
p^{*}\geq 1/2
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}-\frac{1}{1-1/2}\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(1-\frac{1}{1+c_{1}/c_{0}}\right)}-2=\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Lemma
B.16
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\geq\frac{1}{1-p^{*}}-\frac{1}{1-1/2}\geq\frac{b_{3}}{2}(u^{*}-0)\kern 5.0pt,

which implies that
0\leq u^{*}\leq\frac{2}{b_{3}}(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1)
.
Since
\psi(u)
is monotone increasing on
[0,\infty)
, we can derive the following upper bound:

\displaystyle\psi(u^{*})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left[\frac{2}{b_{3}}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)\right]^{2}+\left[\frac{2}{b_{3}}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)\right]^{3}\kern 5.0pt.

For
p^{*}\leq 1/2
, we can derive the same upper bound by symmetry.
∎

Based on Proposition
4.4
, Lemma
4.5
, and Lemma
B.21
, we can finally establish the upper bound for the probability regret in the following proposition.

Proposition
4.7
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, the expected probability regret is bounded in expectation as

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]\leq\max\{c_{2},1\}

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{8}{b_{3}^{3}}\left[\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{3}+\frac{b_{3}}{4}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}c_{1}^{4}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}+o(1)\Bigg)T^{1/2}R^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
4.6
*, Proposition
4.4
, Lemma
4.5
and Lemma
B.21
, we can derive the following upper bound

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta_{T}}\psi(u^{*})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{(\nabla\widehat{h}_{t}(u_{t}))^{2}}{2(1+|u_{t}|)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta_{T}}\cdot\frac{8}{b_{3}^{3}}\left[\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{3}+\frac{b_{3}}{4}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}\left[\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}+\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{4}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R_{T}^{2}T^{1/2}\cdot\frac{8}{b_{3}^{3}}\left[\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{3}+\frac{b_{3}}{4}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}c_{1}^{4}\max\{c_{2},1\}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}R^{2}T^{1/2}+o(R^{2}T^{1/2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\{c_{2},1\}\left(\frac{8}{b_{3}^{3}}\left[\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{3}+\frac{b_{3}}{4}\left(\frac{c_{1}}{c_{0}}-1\right)^{2}\right]+b_{1}^{2}\max\{b_{1},2\}c_{1}^{4}\left(1+2^{-1/2}(\gamma_{0}\vee 4c_{2})^{1/2}\right)^{4}+o(1)\right)T^{1/2}R^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

B.5
Prediction Regret

In this subsection, we derive an upper bound for the expected prediction regret.
The following lemma establish the connection between the defined prediction regret with the corresponding terms that are actually utilized in proposed algorithm.

Lemma
4.8
.

For each iteration
t
, the following conditional unbiasedness holds:

\Esym\big[\widehat{\ell}_{t}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\big\rparen-\widehat{\ell}_{t}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\big\rparen\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\big]=\ell_{t}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\big\rparen-\ell_{t}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\big\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By the definition of
\widehat{\ell}_{t}
and
\ell_{t}
, since
\beta_{t}(1)
and
\beta_{t}(0)
are measurable with respect to
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}[\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\mathrm{E}\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}y_{t}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}y_{t}^{2}(0)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}y_{t}(1)(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}y_{t}(0)(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}y_{t}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}y_{t}^{2}(0)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}y_{t}(1)(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}y_{t}(0)(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Under Lemma
4.8
, the following lemma establishes that the regret can be bounded by a regularization term and the expectation of the sum of a sequence of successive differences.
The derivation follows the standard FTRL analysis, but is adapted to our setting where the step size varies over time.

Lemma
4.9
.

The expected prediction regret is bounded as

\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{pred}}_{T}]\leq\frac{m\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\big\rparen}{\eta_{T+1}}+\Esym\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\tilde{L}_{t+1}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\big\rparen-\tilde{L}_{t+1}\big\lparen\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(0)\big\rparen\Bigg]\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We first prove that
(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))
is the minimizer of
\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))
. For simplicity, for any
t\in[T]
, we denote
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)=\left(y_{1}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{1}=1]}{p_{1}},\ldots,y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\right)
and
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)=\left(y_{1}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{1}=0]}{1-p_{1}},\ldots,y_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\right)
.
Let
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}
.
We further denote
\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}=\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(1)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)
,
\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}=\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(0)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)
,
\boldsymbol{z}_{1}=\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{z}_{0}=\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}
.
Hence we have

\displaystyle\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}-\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\|^{2}\right)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}-\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}-\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}-\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)+\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\rangle\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\|^{2}\right)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right).

This implies that

\displaystyle\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})-\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|^{2}-2\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|^{2}-2\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0),\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(-\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{0}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{\alpha}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\Big(-2\left(\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(1)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(1)\right)^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}

\displaystyle+2\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\Big(-2\left(\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(0)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(0)\right)^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}

\displaystyle+2\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(0)\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\Big(-\left(\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(1)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(1)\right)^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\Big(\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\Big(-\left(\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(0)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}(0)\right)^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\Big(\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(0)\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\left(\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\rangle\right)\kern 5.0pt.

(20)

Note that

\displaystyle-\left(\boldsymbol{I}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})-\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{0}.

Hence (
B.5
) can be further simplifies and lower bounded by:

\displaystyle\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})-\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\|^{2}\Big)+2\left(\langle\boldsymbol{z}_{1},\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1},\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{z}_{1}+\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{z}_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\left\|\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{1}+\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{\delta}_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 0.

Hence we proved that
(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))
is the minimizer of
\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))
.
Similarly we can prove that
(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}(0))
is the minimizer of
\tilde{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))
. Furthermore, we can see that the penalty term is nonnegative:

\displaystyle m(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))=\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)\|^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\|^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\rangle=\left\|\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{\beta}(1)+\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{\beta}(0)\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq 0.

By the construction method, we can easily see that the step size
\eta_{t}
is decreasing with respect to
t
.
By Lemma
4.8
, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right]\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:pred-regret-unbiased}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\widehat{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))+\widehat{L}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1}(0))-\widehat{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(0))\right]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(0))-\widehat{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\widehat{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(0))\right)\right]

(The last inequality is due to the optimality of
(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}(0))
.)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(0)))\right]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(0))-\widehat{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(0))\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(0)))\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t}^{-1}-\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\right)\mathrm{E}\left[m(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}(0))\right]\quad\text{(optimality of $(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(0))$)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\tilde{L}_{t+1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(0)))\right]\kern 5.0pt,

where the last inequality is due to the monotonicity of
\eta_{t}
and the nonnegativity of
m(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))
.
∎

Lemma
4.10
.

The successive difference can be written as:

\tilde{L}_{t+1}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\big\rparen-\tilde{L}_{t+1}\big\lparen\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(0)\big\rparen=\frac{\Pi_{t,t}}{1+\Pi_{t,t}}\cdot\widehat{\ell}_{t}\big\lparen\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\big\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We first derive the explicit form of:

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}(1)\|_{2}^{2}\right]-\left[\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

For notation simplicity, we slightly abuse the notation and suppress
y_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}
as
y_{t}
.
Let
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}=(y_{1},\ldots,y_{t-1})^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
and
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}=(y_{1},\ldots,y_{t})^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
.
Then we have the explicit form for the regression coefficient:
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)=(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}
and
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)=(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}
. Now we calculate the explicit form for
\tilde{L}_{t+1}^{(1)}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1))-\tilde{L}_{t+1}^{(1)}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1))
.

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{W}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle h=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{H}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Step 1:
Calculate
\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}
.

By the explicit form of
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(y_{s}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}}-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})^{2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}-2\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}-2\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{t}-\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\\
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\end{bmatrix}(\boldsymbol{W}+\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\end{bmatrix}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\\
y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\kern 5.0pt.

(21)

By Sherman-Morrison formula, we have

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{W}+\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})^{-1}=\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}-\frac{\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}}{1+\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}}=\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}-\frac{\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}}{1+h}\kern 5.0pt.

(22)

For simplicity, suppose we have the following block form for the matrix:

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\\
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\end{bmatrix}(\boldsymbol{W}+\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{R}_{1,1}&\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}\\
\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&R_{2,2}\end{bmatrix}\kern 5.0pt.

Now we calculate the explicit form for each block:

(1)

By (
22
),
\boldsymbol{R}_{1,1}
can calculated as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{1,1}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}-\frac{\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}}{1+h}=\boldsymbol{H}-\frac{\boldsymbol{\alpha}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}}{1+h}\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

By (
22
),
\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}
can be calculated as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}=\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\frac{\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}}{1+h}=\boldsymbol{\alpha}-\frac{h}{1+h}\boldsymbol{\alpha}=\frac{1}{1+h}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\kern 5.0pt.

(3)

By (
22
),
R_{2,2}
can be calculated as:

\displaystyle R_{2,2}=\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\frac{\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}}{1+h}=h-\frac{h^{2}}{1+h}=\frac{h}{1+h}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by (
B.5
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{t}-\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\\
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\end{bmatrix}(\boldsymbol{W}+\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}})^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\end{bmatrix}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\\
y_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{t}-\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{R}_{1,1}&\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}\\
\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&R_{2,2}\\
\end{bmatrix}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\\
y_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{R}_{1,1}&-\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}\\
-\boldsymbol{R}_{1,2}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&1-R_{2,2}\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\\
y_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&y_{t}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{H})+\frac{\boldsymbol{\alpha}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}}{1+h}&-\frac{1}{1+h}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\\
-\frac{1}{1+h}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}&\frac{1}{1+h}\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\\
y_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{H})\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}+\frac{1}{1+h}(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1})^{2}-\frac{2y_{t}}{1+h}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}+\frac{1}{1+h}y_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{H})\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}+\frac{1}{1+h}(y_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1})^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

(23)

Step 2:
Calculate
\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}(1)\|_{2}^{2}
.

Since
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)=(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d})^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}=\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\widehat{\ell}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1))+\eta_{t}^{-1}\|{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t}(1)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+(y_{t}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{W}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1})^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{H})^{2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}+(y_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1})^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left[(\boldsymbol{I}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{H})^{2}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{W}^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right]\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1}+(y_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t-1})^{2}

\displaystyle=
