\displaystyle\left(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\right)\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Pi_{t,t}(t-1)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}(t-1)^{1/2}T^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1} and Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1/2}T^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)R_{t}T^{1/4}\kern 5.0pt.

(56)

Hence by (

), Corollary

,

can be bounded as:

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{s}(1)\rangle^{4}\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{s}}\right)^{1/2}(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{t}(0))

\displaystyle(2^{1/2}+1)^{2}c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)T^{5/4}R_{t}(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0))~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.4}})\kern 5.0pt.

For

For

\displaystyle+2\operatorname{Var}\left[\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle 2\sum_{r=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{s=r+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,r}^{2}\right)^{2}y_{r}^{4}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{r}=1]}{p_{r}}-1\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{s=t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}^{2}\right)y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\operatorname{Var}\left[\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)\right]

(64)

For any

, if the maximum among

is unique (assume without loss of generality that

is the unique maximum), by the law of total variance, there holds:

\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{4}}=1]}{p_{t_{4}}}-1\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{4}}=1]}{p_{t_{4}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-1}\right),\mathrm{E}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{4}}=1]}{p_{t_{4}}}-1\right)\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

Hence the only nonzero terms in the expansion of
B_{3}
have four indices where exact two numbers attain the maximum number among the four indices. Then we can rewrite
B_{3}
as

\displaystyle B_{3}\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y^{2}_{t_{2}}(1)\operatorname{Var}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle+4\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right)y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)y_{t_{3}}(1)\right|

\displaystyle\times\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right),\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}-1\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle 2B_{3,1}+4B_{3,2}\kern 5.0pt.

By Corollary
B.4
and Lemma
B.9
, for any
1\leq s\leq t-1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}R_{r}^{2}R_{s}^{2}((r-1)\vee\eta_{r}^{-1})^{-2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}R_{r}^{4}((r-1)\vee\eta_{r}^{-1})^{-2}~~~~(\text{by $R_{s}\leq R_{r}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}((r-1)R_{r}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}((r-1)R_{t}^{-2}\vee T^{1/2})^{-2}~~~~(\text{by $R_{r}\leq R_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}R_{t}^{4}((r-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}\xi_{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.3}})\kern 5.0pt.

Then by Lemma

, Lemma

\displaystyle B_{3,1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y^{2}_{t_{2}}(1)\operatorname{Var}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}\vee t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y^{2}_{t_{2}}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}-1\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq t-1}\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{s=t_{2}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}^{2}\right)^{2}y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y^{2}_{t_{2}}(1)\right)\cdot T^{9/32}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.12}})

\displaystyle R_{t}^{8}\eta_{t}^{2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{r=s+1}^{t}\Pi_{r,s}^{2}\right)y^{2}_{s}(1)\right)^{2}\cdot T^{9/32}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T^{5/4}R_{t}^{3})~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq t-1}\left|\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{2}}\right)\left(\sum_{s=t_{1}+1}^{t}\Pi_{s,t_{1}}\Pi_{s,t_{3}}\right)y^{2}_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)y_{t_{3}}(1)\right|

\displaystyle\times\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{25/16}\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{15/32}\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{15/96}\left(\frac{R_{t}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{15/96}

\displaystyle 72c_{1}^{4}(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}R_{t}^{4}\eta_{t}^{1/2}T^{3/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\cdot\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{25/16}

Lemma
C.3
establishes that the variance of
\widehat{a}_{t}(1)
(or
\widehat{A}_{t}(1)
) and
\widehat{a}_{t}(0)
(or
\widehat{A}_{t}(0)
) are upper bounded in a mutually normalizing manner. Such dependence can be undesirable, as the estimated squared residuals for each group may fail to concentrate around their respective expectations when the treatment group and controlled group are imbalanced. Nevertheless, as implied by Corollary
B.17
and Lemma
C.1
, once the expected squared residuals exceed a certain threshold, the maximal (positive) deviation between the two groups is controlled, thereby ensuring a degree of concentration despite the mutual normalization.
The result is formally stated in the following lemma.

Lemma C.4
.

Suppose
T
is large enough.
Suppose
0<\delta<\frac{1}{4}
is a fixed constant and
\tilde{C}>0
is a fixed constant given by

\displaystyle\tilde{C}=

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\left[\frac{C^{1/2}b_{1}^{25/32}b_{2}^{281/128}}{b_{3}}+\frac{2^{57/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{89/32}b_{2}^{185/128}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad 2^{283/64}C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256},~\frac{2^{57/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{89/32}b_{2}^{185/128}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right),

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\frac{2^{89/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{121/32}b_{2}^{217/128}}{b_{3}^{5/2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right),~2^{89/16}Cb_{1}^{41/16}b_{2}^{41/64},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}},~33C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256}\Bigg\}\kern 5.0pt.

Then under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, we have the following upper bound:

(1)

For any
t\in[T]
such that
a_{t}(1)\geq 5b_{1}^{-4}b_{2}^{-1}
, there holds

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{C}\max\Bigg\{(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1),

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{89/128}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-3/2}(1)

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-2}a_{t}^{-2}(1)(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{41/64},~\delta+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1)\Bigg\}\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

For any
t\in[T]
such that
a_{t}(0)\geq 5b_{1}^{-4}b_{2}^{-1}
, there holds

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{C}\max\Bigg\{(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(0)+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(0),

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(0)+(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{89/128}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-3/2}(0)

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-2}a_{t}^{-2}(0)(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{41/64},~\delta+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(0)\Bigg\}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove the first part.
For any
t\in[T]
such that
a_{t}(1)\geq 5b_{1}^{-4}b_{2}^{-1}
, it is easy to see that
2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)\leq 2b_{1}b_{2}^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)
.
We then consider the following three cases:

(1)

a_{t}(0)\leq(1-\delta)a_{t}(1)
.

By Chebyshev’s inequality and Lemma
C.3
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}\left(\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\leq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}\left(\frac{|\widehat{a}_{t}(1)-a_{t}(1)|}{a_{t}(1)}+\frac{|\widehat{a}_{t}(0)-a_{t}(0)|}{a_{t}(1)}\geq\delta\right)\quad(\text{Chebyshev's inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}\left(\frac{|\widehat{a}_{t}(1)-a_{t}(1)|}{a_{t}(1)}\geq\frac{\delta}{2}\right)+\operatorname{Pr}\left(\frac{|\widehat{a}_{t}(0)-a_{t}(0)|}{a_{t}(1)}\geq\frac{\delta}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\operatorname{Var}\left(\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\right)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}+\frac{4\operatorname{Var}\left(\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\right)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4C(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{25/16}}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}

\displaystyle+\frac{4C(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)\right)^{25/16}}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8C(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)\right)^{25/16}}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\cdot 2^{25/16}Cb_{1}^{25/16}b_{2}^{25/64}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{25/64}(1)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{2}(1)}\quad(\text{since $a_{t}(0)\leq a_{t}(1)$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2^{73/16}Cb_{1}^{25/16}b_{2}^{25/64}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{103/64}(1)}\kern 5.0pt.

(69)

Under event
\{\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\geq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\}
, we have
p_{t+1},\bar{p}_{t+1}\geq 1/2
. By definition,
p_{t+1}
and

satisfy:

\displaystyle-\frac{a_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{\bar{p}_{t+1}^{2}}+\frac{a_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{(1-\bar{p}_{t+1})^{2}}+\Psi^{\prime}(\bar{p}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle-\frac{\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{p_{t+1}^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{(1-p_{t+1})^{2}}+\Psi^{\prime}(p_{t+1})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt.

By Lemma
4.14
and Lemma
C.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}\frac{|a_{t}(1)-\widehat{a}_{t}(1)|\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{a_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}+\frac{b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\frac{\bar{p}_{t+1}}{1-\bar{p}_{t+1}}\cdot\frac{|a_{t}(0)-\widehat{a}_{t}(0)|\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{a_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}\frac{|a_{t}(1)-\widehat{a}_{t}(1)|\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}{a_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\frac{\left(1+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)\cdot\left(\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\eta_{t}}\right)^{1/4}\right)|a_{t}(0)-\widehat{a}_{t}(0)|}{a_{t}(1)}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:effect-of-p-regularization}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}\frac{|a_{t}(1)-\widehat{a}_{t}(1)|}{a_{t}(1)}+\frac{2b_{1}^{2}b_{2}^{5/4}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\frac{|a_{t}(0)-\widehat{a}_{t}(0)|}{a_{t}^{3/4}(1)}\quad(\text{since $\eta_{t+1}\leq\eta_{t}$})\kern 5.0pt.

(70)

By calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\mathbf{1}\left[\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\geq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\right]\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\mathbf{1}\left[\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\leq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\right]\right]

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle D_{1}+D_{2}.

(71)

For term
D_{1}
, by Lemma
C.3
, (
(1)
) and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle D_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}\frac{|a_{t}(1)-\widehat{a}_{t}(1)|}{a_{t}(1)}+\frac{2b_{1}^{2}b_{2}^{5/4}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\frac{|a_{t}(0)-\widehat{a}_{t}(0)|}{a_{t}^{3/4}(1)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}a_{t}^{-1}(1)\operatorname{Var}^{1/2}(\widehat{a}_{t}(1))+\frac{2b_{1}^{2}b_{2}^{5/4}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)a_{t}^{-3/4}(1)\operatorname{Var}^{1/2}(\widehat{a}_{t}(0))\quad(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C^{1/2}b_{2}^{2}}{2b_{3}}a_{t}^{-1}(1)(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{25/32}

\displaystyle+\frac{2C^{1/2}b_{1}^{2}b_{2}^{5/4}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)a_{t}^{-3/4}(1)(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)\right)^{25/32}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2^{-7/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{25/32}b_{2}^{281/128}}{b_{3}}a_{t}^{-1}(1)(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{25/128}(1)

\displaystyle+\frac{2^{57/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{89/32}b_{2}^{185/128}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)a_{t}^{-3/4}(1)(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{25/128}(1)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\frac{1}{2}-p_{t+1}}{\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(1-p_{t+1}\right)}\right|+\left|\frac{\frac{1}{2}-\bar{p}_{t+1}}{\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(1-\bar{p}_{t+1}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}-2\right|+\left|\frac{1}{1-\bar{p}_{t+1}}-2\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly we can prove that under event
\{a_{t}(1)\geq a_{t}(0)\}\cap\{\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\leq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\}
or event
\{a_{t}(1)\leq a_{t}(0)\}\cap\{\widehat{a}_{t}(1)\geq\widehat{a}_{t}(0)\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\leq\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta\kern 5.0pt.

(84)

Since
\delta<1/4
, by (
(3)
), (
(3)
), (
84
), Corollary (
4.15
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\mathbf{1}\left[|\widehat{a}_{t}(1)-a_{t}(1)|\leq\delta a_{t}(0)\right]\cdot\mathbf{1}\left[|\widehat{a}_{t}(0)-a_{t}(0)|\leq\delta a_{t}(1)\right]\right]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}+\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right)\mathbf{1}\left[|\widehat{a}_{t}(1)-a_{t}(1)|\geq\delta a_{t}(0)\right]\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}+\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right)\mathbf{1}\left[|\widehat{a}_{t}(0)-a_{t}(0)|\geq\delta a_{t}(1)\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta+\left(\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\left(\frac{1}{p_{t+1}^{4}}\right)+\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\left(\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}^{4}}\right)\right)\left(\operatorname{Pr}\left(|\widehat{a}_{t}(1)-a_{t}(1)|\geq\delta a_{t}(0)\right)\right)^{3/4}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\left(\frac{1}{p_{t+1}^{4}}\right)+\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\left(\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}^{4}}\right)\right)\left(\operatorname{Pr}\left(|\widehat{a}_{t}(0)-a_{t}(0)|\geq\delta a_{t}(1)\right)\right)^{3/4}\quad(\text{by \eqref{lem5.5_eq16} and H{\"{o}}lder's inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\left(\frac{2^{25/16}Cb_{1}^{25/16}b_{2}^{25/64}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{103/64}(1)}\right)^{3/4}+\left(\frac{2^{41/16}Cb_{1}^{25/16}b_{2}^{25/64}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)}{\delta^{2}a_{t}^{103/64}(1)}\right)^{3/4}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left(2\cdot\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)\right)+\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta\quad(\text{by \eqref{lem5.5_eq14}, \eqref{lem5.5_eq15} and Corollary \eqref{corollary:p-moment}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta+8b_{1}b_{2}^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\cdot\frac{2^{123/64}C^{3/4}b_{1}^{75/64}b_{2}^{75/256}(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)}{\delta^{3/2}a_{t}^{309/256}(1)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta+2^{315/64}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256}(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-309/256}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta+2^{315/64}(1-\delta)^{-1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(1)C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256}(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-309/256}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}}\delta+33C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256}(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1)\kern 5.0pt.

(85)

Hence by (
(1)
), (
(2)
) and (
(3)
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{C}\max\Bigg\{(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1),

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{89/128}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-3/2}(1)

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-2}a_{t}^{-2}(1)(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{41/64},~\delta+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1)\Bigg\}\kern 5.0pt,

where

\displaystyle\tilde{C}=

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\left[\frac{C^{1/2}b_{1}^{25/32}b_{2}^{281/128}}{b_{3}}+\frac{2^{57/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{89/32}b_{2}^{185/128}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad 2^{283/64}C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256},~\frac{2^{57/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{89/32}b_{2}^{185/128}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right),

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\frac{2^{89/32}C^{1/2}b_{1}^{121/32}b_{2}^{217/128}}{b_{3}^{5/2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right),~2^{89/16}Cb_{1}^{41/16}b_{2}^{41/64},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\frac{8b_{1}b_{2}}{b_{3}},~33C^{3/4}b_{1}^{139/64}b_{2}^{139/256}\Bigg\}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

By selecting an appropriate constant
\delta>0
and a suitable threshold in Lemma
C.4
, we obtain the following simplified result stated in the corollary.

Corollary C.5
.

Suppose
T
is large enough. Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there exist a constant
K_{2}>0
such that:

(1)

For any
t\in[T]
such that
a_{t}(1)\geq(\eta_{t}T)^{7/10}
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]\leq K_{2}(\eta_{t}T)^{-2/25}\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

For any
t\in[T]
such that
a_{t}(0)\geq(\eta_{t}T)^{7/10}
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{1-\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]\leq K_{2}(\eta_{t}T)^{-2/25}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We only prove the first part. It is easy to see that when
T
is large enough,
(\eta_{t}T)^{7/10}\geq(\eta_{T}T)^{7/10}\geq 5b_{1}^{-4}b_{2}^{-1}
. For
0<\delta<1/4
, by Lemma
C.4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{C}\max\Bigg\{(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1),

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-71/128}(1)+(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{89/128}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\log(\eta_{t}T)a_{t}^{-3/2}(1)

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-2}a_{t}^{-2}(1)(\eta_{t}\kappa)^{41/64},~\delta+(\eta_{t}T)^{3/8}\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)\delta^{-3/2}a_{t}^{-245/256}(1)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\tilde{C}\max\{B_{1},B_{2},B_{3}\}\kern 5.0pt.

By Corollary
B.17
, we have
\eta_{t}\kappa\lesssim\eta_{t}T
. Then by choosing
\delta=(\eta_{t}T)^{-2/25}
, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\log(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{71}{128}\cdot\frac{7}{10}}+\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{3}{2}\cdot\frac{2}{25}+\frac{3}{8}-\frac{245}{256}\cdot\frac{7}{10}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{-\frac{2}{25}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle B_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\log(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{71}{128}\cdot\frac{7}{10}}+\log(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{89}{128}+\frac{1}{4}-\frac{3}{2}\cdot\frac{7}{10}}+\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{2\cdot\frac{2}{25}+\frac{1}{2}-2\cdot\frac{7}{10}+\frac{41}{64}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{-\frac{2}{25}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle B_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{-\frac{2}{25}}+\log^{3/2}(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{3}{2}\cdot\frac{2}{25}+\frac{3}{8}-\frac{245}{256}\cdot\frac{7}{10}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\eta_{t}T)^{-\frac{2}{25}}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence there exists constant
K_{2}>0
(independent of
t
and
T
) such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t+1}}\right|\right]\leq\max\{B_{1},B_{2},B_{3}\}\leq K_{2}(\eta_{t}T)^{-2/25}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Based on Corollary
C.5
, the following lemma verifies the first convergence result in (
C.2.1
).

Lemma C.6
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{G}=\left\{2\leq t\leq T:a_{t-1}(1)\geq(\eta_{t-1}T)^{7/10}\right\}
. Then we can obtain the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in\bar{\mathcal{G}}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in{\mathcal{G}}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle B_{1}+B_{2}\kern 5.0pt.

(86)

Define
t_{0}=1
and suppose
\bar{\mathcal{G}}=\{t_{1},\ldots,t_{N}\}
, where
t_{1}<\ldots<t_{N}
. For any
t\in[T]
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, (
C.2.1
), Corollary
B.4
, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
, Assumption
1
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]-\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t-1}(1)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}+\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s}}-1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle y_{t}^{2}(1)+\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{2}+R_{t}((t-1)\vee\eta_{t}^{-1})^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}|\Pi_{t,s}|R_{s}y_{s}^{2}(1)\eta_{s}^{1/4}T^{1/4}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}, \ref{corollary:p-moment} and \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+R_{t}T^{1/4}+R_{t}\eta_{t}T^{1/8}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}|\Pi_{t,s}|R_{s}\cdot R_{s}^{-1/2}y_{s}^{2}(1)~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments} and \eqref{lem5.2_eq}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+T^{1/2}(TR_{t}^{-4})^{-1/4}+T^{1/8-1/2}R_{t}^{1-2+1-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+T^{1/8-1/2+1/2}R_{t}^{-1/2}\Pi_{t,t}^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments} and Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+T^{1/8}R_{t}^{-1/2+1}\eta_{t}^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+T^{1/8-1/4}R_{t}^{-1/2+1-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}+T^{-1/8}R_{t}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence there exists constant
K_{3}>0
, such that
\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]-\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t-1}(1)]\leq K_{3}T^{1/2}
for any
t\in[T]
.
Moreover, the proof also establishes that
(y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\leq\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]-\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t-1}(1)]
.
Hence by the definition of
\bar{\mathcal{G}}
, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\in\bar{\mathcal{G}}}\left(\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t}(1)]-\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t-1}(1)]\right)(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}\quad(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left(\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{k}}(1)]-\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{k-1}}(1)]\right)(\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{k}}(1)]\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)+T^{-1}\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{N}}(1)](\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}-T^{-1}\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{0}}(1)](\eta_{t_{1}}T)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\left(\eta_{t_{k}-1}^{-1}\cdot\eta_{t_{k}-1}\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{k}-1}(1)]+K_{3}T^{1/2}\right)\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\left(\eta_{t_{N}-1}^{-1}\cdot\eta_{t_{N}-1}\Esym[\widehat{A}_{t_{N}-1}(1)]+K_{3}T^{1/2}\right)(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\eta_{t_{k}-1}^{-1}(\eta_{t_{k}-1}T)^{7/10}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)+T^{-1}\eta_{t_{N}-1}^{-1}(\eta_{t_{N}-1}T)^{7/10}(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+K_{3}T^{-1/2}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)+(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}\right]~~~~(\text{by the definition of $\bar{\mathcal{G}}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{3}T^{-1/2}(\eta_{t_{1}}T)^{1/4}+T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\eta_{t_{k}}^{-1}(\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{7/10}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)+T^{-1}\eta_{t_{N}}^{-1}(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{7/10}(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{3}T^{-1/2+1/8}+(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{-1+7/10+1/4}+\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}(\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{-1+7/10}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o(1)+(\eta_{T}T)^{-1/20}+\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}\right)^{-6/5}\left((\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}-(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}\right)~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o(1)+\sum_{k=1}^{N-1}\int_{(\eta_{t_{k+1}}T)^{1/4}}^{(\eta_{t_{k}}T)^{1/4}}x^{-6/5}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o(1)+\int_{(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{1/4}}^{(\eta_{t_{1}}T)^{1/4}}x^{-6/5}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle o(1)+(\eta_{t_{N}}T)^{-\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o(1)+(\eta_{T}T)^{-1/20}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

(87)

By the definition of set
\mathcal{G}
and Corollary
C.5
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in\mathcal{G}}\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{t\in\mathcal{G}}(\eta_{t-1}T)^{-2/25}\leq(\eta_{T}T)^{-2/25}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Lemma
B.11
and Assumption
3
, we can bound
B_{2}
by:

\displaystyle B_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in\mathcal{G}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\in\mathcal{G}}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}(\eta_{T}T)^{-2/25}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}\cdot T(\eta_{T}T)^{-2/25}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{coro4.3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\eta_{T}T)^{-2/25}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

(88)

Hence by (
C.2.1
), (
C.2.1
), (
C.2.1
), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right)=B_{1}+B_{2}\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

(89)

∎

Before verifying the remaining convergence results in (
C.2.1
), we first establish the explicit form of the tracking error terms.

Lemma C.7
.

The expected prediction tracking terms can be computed as

\displaystyle\Esym\Big[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\Big]=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\Esym\Big[\frac{1}{p_{s}}-1\Big]\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Esym\Big[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}\Big]=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(0)\Esym\Big[\frac{1}{1-p_{s}}-1\Big]\kern 5.0pt.

The remaining convergence results in (
C.2.1
) are easier to establish using the previous lemmas.

Lemma C.8
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
B.15
, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
, Proposition
5.4
and Lemma
C.7
, We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+7/26}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-4/11}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]~~~~(\text{Proposition \ref{prop:as-inv-prob-bound}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1+7/26}\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-4/11}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s}}-1\right]~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma-tracking-term}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+7/26+1/4}\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-4/11}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\eta_{s}^{1/4}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+7/26+1/4-1/8}\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-4/11}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}R_{s}^{-1/2}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+7/26+1/4-1/8}\cdot T^{3/4-(4/11+1/2)/4}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.9}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-41/572}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

(90)

∎

Lemma C.9
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}-1\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

Note that the almost sure bound in Proposition
5.4
also serves as an upper bound for
1/\bar{p}_{t}
. Then by the same proof in Lemma
C.8
, the result is proved.
∎

Lemma C.10
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
C.6
, Lemma
C.8
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Lemma C.11
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}-1\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma
B.11
, Lemma
B.15
, Lemma
C.7
, Lemma
4.14
and Corollary
B.17
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}|y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle|\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}-1\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}|y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle|\cdot\eta_{t}^{1/4}T^{1/4}\right]~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:effect-of-p-regularization} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/8}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-1/2}|y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+1/8}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+1/8+1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s}}-1\right]\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{coro4.3} and Lemma \ref{lemma-tracking-term}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+1/8+1/2+1/16}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}R_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}R_{s}^{-1/2}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:effect-of-p-regularization} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+1/8+1/2+1/16}\cdot\left(T^{3/4-(1+1/2)/4}\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lem4.9}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1/8}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

(91)

∎

Based on Lemma
C.6
-
C.11
, we now verify the first convergence result in (
C.2.1
) in the following lemma.

Lemma C.12
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-1\right)-\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-1\right)\right]\right]\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)-\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)\right]\right]\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Markov’s inequality and Lemma
C.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right)\right|\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt.

On the other hand, we also have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right)\right]\right|\leq\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

These two convergence results imply that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right)-\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{t}}\right)\right]\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt.

By Lemma
C.8
, Lemma
C.9
, Lemma
C.10
and Lemma
C.11
, we can similarly prove the other four convergence results, which verifies the following:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-1\right)-\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}^{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{p_{t}}-1\right)\right]\right]\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt.

The second convergence is proved due to the symmetry.
∎

We can similarly verify the final convergence result in (
C.2.1
) in the following lemma.

Lemma C.13
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}-\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right\}\left\{y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right\}\right]\right]\xrightarrow{p}0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We proof the result by similar method as in Lemma
C.12
.
First, we decompose the random term:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\kern 5.0pt.

Note that the first term is nonrandom. Hence by Markov’s inequality, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle|\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

By Lemma
C.7
, Lemma
B.15
, Lemma
4.14
and Corollary
B.17
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s}}-1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/8}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}R_{s}^{-1/2}\Pi_{t,s}^{2}y_{s}^{2}(1)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/8}\cdot T^{3/4-1/2/4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{3/4}\kern 5.0pt.

(92)

A similar result can be derived for
\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}\right]
.
Hence by (
C.2.1
), Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma
B.11
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right||\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathrm{E}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}\right)^{1/2}~~~~(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz inequality})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1+3/8}\cdot\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\quad(\text{by \eqref{thm5.1_eq26}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-5/8}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-5/8}\cdot\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-5/8+1/2}~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{coro4.3} and \eqref{thm5.1_eq26}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1/8}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

(93)

Hence the result is proved by similar argument as in Lemma
C.12
.
∎

By Lemma
C.12
and Lemma
C.13
, we can verify the stable variance condition in the following lemma:

Lemma C.14
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
and Condition
1
, there holds:
V_{T}^{2}\xrightarrow{p}1
.

Proof.

By Lemma
C.12
, Lemma
C.13
and the simplified form of
V_{T}^{2}
, the result is proved.
∎

C.2.2
Conditional Lyapunov Condition

We first derive a simplified form of the conditional Lyapunov condition.
Setting
\delta=2
, we obtain the following result by applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Esym[X_{t,T}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1,T}]\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{4}(\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen)^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)^{4}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1,T}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{4}(\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen)^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}-1\right)^{4}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1,T}\right]\quad(\text{Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{4}(\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen)^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}+\big\lparen y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-p_{t})^{3}}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

By Markov’s inequality and non-superefficiency condition (Corollary
5.2
), it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-p_{t})^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Owing to the symmetry between the treated and control groups, we only need to prove the first convergence.
Using the inequality
(a+b)^{4}\leq 8a^{4}+8b^{4}
, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0.

(94)

The two convergence results in (
C.2.2
) are proved separately in Lemma
C.15
and Lemma
C.18
.

Lemma C.15
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
B.7
, Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t}T)^{3/4}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}\quad(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-2+3/4}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}^{-1/4}\cdot\eta_{t}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-5/4}\eta_{T}^{-1/4}\cdot R_{T}^{2}T^{1/2}\quad(\text{by Lemma \ref{lem4.5} and $\eta_{T}\leq\eta_{t}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-5/8}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0\quad(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Before verifying the second convergence result in (
C.2.2
), we first establish the following result, which is a direct corollary of Lemma
C.3
.

Corollary C.16
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for any
t\in[T]
, there holds:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}^{2}(1),\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}^{2}(0)\right\}\leq\tilde{\kappa}\kern 5.0pt,

where
\tilde{\kappa}=(4c_{1}^{4}+o(1))T^{2}
is a constant that does not depend on
t
.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}^{2}(1)
.
By Lemma
C.3
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\Varsym\lparen\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\rparen\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-2}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)\right)^{25/16}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{2}\cdot(\eta_{t}T)^{-2}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/2}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)(\eta_{t}T)^{\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{25}{16}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{2}\cdot(\eta_{t}T)^{-71/64}\log^{2}(\eta_{t}T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T^{2})\quad(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Corollary
B.17
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}^{2}(1)=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{E}\widehat{A}_{t}(1))^{2}+\Varsym\lparen\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\rparen\leq(2c_{1}^{2}+o(1))^{2}T^{2}+o(T^{2})=(4c_{1}^{4}+o(1))T^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Based on Corollary
C.16
, we refine the result of Lemma
B.18
in the following lemma.

Lemma C.17
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{81/512}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Corollary
C.16
and similar method in Corollary
4.15
, we can prove that for any
0\leq k\leq 8
and any
t\in[T]
, there holds:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{t}^{k}}\right],\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{(1-p_{t})^{k}}\right]\right\}\leq(\eta_{t}T)^{k/4}\kern 5.0pt.

By this result, we can sharpen the uniform upper bound derived in Lemma
B.18
.
By similar method as in the proof of Lemma
B.18
, we can show

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{1/8}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}^{8/7}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{8/7}}\right]\right)^{7/8}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{1/8}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{1/7}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{8/7}}\right]\right)^{7/8}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{1/8}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{1}{56}}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}^{\frac{8}{7}\cdot\frac{56}{55}}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{55}{56}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}<s_{2}<s_{1}\leq T}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{1}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{1/8}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{2}}^{8}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{1}{56}}\left(\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{s_{3}}^{\frac{9}{55}}}\right]\right)^{\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{55}{56}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\eta_{1}T\right)^{\left(8\cdot\frac{1}{8}+8\cdot\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{1}{56}+\frac{9}{55}\cdot\frac{7}{8}\cdot\frac{55}{56}\right)/4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\eta_{1}T\right)^{81/256}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq s_{3}\neq s_{2}\neq s_{1}\leq T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{1}}=1]}{p_{s_{1}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{2}}=1]}{p_{s_{2}}}-1\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{s_{3}}=1]}{p_{s_{3}}}-1\right)\right]\right|=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{81/512}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

∎

We now verify the second convergence result in (
C.2.2
) with the help of Lemma
C.17
.

Lemma C.18
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]\rightarrow 0\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Assumption
3
, Proposition
5.4
and similar method as in the proof of Corollary
B.19
using the refined bound in Lemma
C.17
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-2+21/26}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[R_{t}^{-12/11}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]\quad(\text{Proposition \ref{prop:as-inv-prob-bound}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-31/26}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[R_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-31/26}\Big(R_{T}^{8}\eta_{T}^{3}T\cdot T^{3/8}+R_{T}^{13/2}\eta_{T}^{7/4}T^{7/8}\cdot T^{5/16}+R_{T}^{6}\eta_{T}^{2}T\cdot T^{9/32}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+R_{T}^{4}\eta_{T}T\cdot T^{81/512}\Big)\quad(\text{using similar proof as in Corollary \ref{coro4.8} and Lemma \ref{lemma:refined-p-bound}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-137/104}\cdot R_{T}^{-85/26}+(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-183/208}\cdot R_{T}^{-27/52}+(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-379/416}\cdot R_{T}^{-171/104}

\displaystyle+(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-3555/6656}\cdot R_{T}^{-227/1664}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(1)\quad(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

∎

Based on Lemma
C.15
and Lemma
C.18
.
We now verify conditional Lyapunov condition.

Lemma C.19
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
and Condition
1
, there holds
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Esym[X_{t,T}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1,T}]\xrightarrow{p}0
.

Proof.

By Corollary
5.2
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and AM-GM inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Esym[X_{t,T}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1,T}]\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{p_{t}^{3}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\big\lparen y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\big\rparen^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-p_{t})^{3}}\right]+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle^{4}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-p_{t})^{3}}\right]\kern 5.0pt,

which should converge to 0 by Lemma
C.15
, Lemma
C.18
and symmetric results regarding the controlled group (
k=0
).
This implies that the conditional Lyapunov condition should hold by Markov’s inequality.
∎

Finally, under Lemma
C.14
and Lemma
C.19
, we can verify the central limit theorem:

Theorem C.20
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
and Condition
1
, the standardized adaptive Horvitz-Thompson estimator is asymptotically standard normal:

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{\tau}-\tau}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau})}}\xrightarrow{d}\mathcal{N}(0,1)\kern 5.0pt.

C.3
Non-superefficiency

In this section, we verify the non-superefficiency condition in Corollary
5.2
.
We first introduce the following notations:

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(0)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle)^{2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\tilde{p}=

\displaystyle\operatorname{argmin}_{p\in(0,1)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Bigg(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{p}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(0)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-p}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi(p)\Bigg)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\breve{p}=

\displaystyle\operatorname{argmin}_{p\in(0,1)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Bigg((y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{p}+(y_{t}(0)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-p}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi(p)\Bigg)\kern 5.0pt.

In Proposition
4.11
, we have shown that the upper bound of the prediction regret is sublinear.
The following lemma further establishes that the lower bound of the prediction regret is also sublinear.

Lemma C.21
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for
k\in\{0,1\}
, there holds:
\Esym[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]=o(T)
.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
k=1
.
By Lemma
4.9
and Assumption
3
, we have already proved that the positive part of
\Esym[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]
is of order
o(T)
.
It suffice to prove that the negative part is also of order
o(T)
.
By direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{2\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)+2\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle+2\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\kern 5.0pt.

(95)

Since
\Esym[\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(k)]=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(k)
for any
k\in\{0,1\}
and
t\in[T]
, terms 2, 5, 8 and 9 in (
C.3
) have expectation as 0. We denote

\displaystyle\bar{L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))=\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\ell_{s}(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))+\eta_{t-1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}(0))

and let
(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t}(0))
denote its minimizer.
Then by (
C.2.1
) and similar calculation as in Lemma
4.9
, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0))-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]+\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0)-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\quad(\text{by \eqref{thm5.1_eq26}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T)+\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))+{L}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle+{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T)+\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\ell}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({L}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))-{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T)+\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(0))-\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0)))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle o(T)+\left(\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t}^{-1}-\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\right)m(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\bar{L}_{t+1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(0))-\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0)))\quad\text{(optimality of $(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{t+1}^{*}(0))$)}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle o(T)+S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}\kern 5.0pt.

(96)

For simplicity, we denote
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)=(y_{1}(1),\ldots,y_{t}(1))^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
and
\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)=(y_{1}(0),\ldots,y_{t}(0))^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
for
t\in[T]
. By direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle S_{1}=

\displaystyle\eta_{T+1}^{-1}m(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))+{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{T+1}^{*}(0))-{L}_{T+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\Big[\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\Big[\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(0)\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\Big]

\displaystyle-2\Big[\langle\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1),\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle-\langle\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1),\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{d}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\rangle

\displaystyle-\eta_{T}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1),\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(0)\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\left[-\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)+\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)\boldsymbol{Y}_{T}(1)\right]
