(97)

For any
t\in[T]
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma
B.3
and Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left\|\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-2}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{s}^{2}(1)\right)\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{s}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}t^{1/2}\left(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1\right)(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1}~~~~\text{(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma \ref{lem4.1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1/2}~~~~\text{(Assumption \ref{assumption:moments})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Similar result can be obtained for
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0)
.
Hence we can bound
-S_{2}
by:

\displaystyle-S_{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t+1}(1)\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t+1}(0)\|_{2}^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t+1}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}_{t+1}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\int_{0}^{\eta_{T+1}^{-1}}x^{-1/2}\mathrm{d}x~~~~\text{($\eta_{t}$ is decreasing)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\eta_{T+1}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\eta_{T}^{-1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

\displaystyle-\left(\langle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\langle\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(0)\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(1)\Big(-2\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}+2\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\Big)\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)\kern 5.0pt.

Hence

\displaystyle\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))-\bar{L}_{t+1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(0))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)+\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)\right]^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\Big(-2\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}+2\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}-\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\left[\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(0)}{\mathcal{E}(1)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(1)+\left(\frac{\mathcal{E}(1)}{\mathcal{E}(0)}\right)^{1/2}\boldsymbol{Y}_{t}(0)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(0)\right)\Big\|\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\Big(-2\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}+2\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\Big)\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(0)\right)\Big\|\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}\Big(-2\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}+2\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\Big)\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(0)\right)\|\boldsymbol{V}\|_{2}\kern 5.0pt.

Let
\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{d}
be all eigenvalues of
\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}
. Since
\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}
,
\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}
and
\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\boldsymbol{I}_{d}
can be simultaneously orthogonally diagonalized, if
\lambda
is the eigenvalue of
\boldsymbol{X}_{t}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{t}
, then the corresponding eigenvalue of
\boldsymbol{V}
is as:

\displaystyle\lambda\left(-\frac{2}{\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}}+\frac{2}{\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1}}-\frac{1}{\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1}}+\frac{\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1}}{(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\cdot\frac{-2(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})(\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1})+(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{2}+(\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}}{(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{2}(\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\cdot\frac{(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\lambda-\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}}{(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{2}(\lambda+\eta_{t}^{-1})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}\frac{1}{(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

By similar proof as in Lemma
B.3
,
(\lambda+\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1}\leq(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1}
, then we have
\|\boldsymbol{V}\|_{2}\leq(\gamma_{0}\vee c_{2}\vee 1)^{2}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-2}
. Hence by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we can bound
S_{3}
as:

\displaystyle-S_{3}=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\bar{L}_{t+1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(1),\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t+1}^{*}(0))-\bar{L}_{t+1}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(1),\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{*}_{t+1}(0))\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{2}(0)\right)\|\boldsymbol{V}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{4}(1)\right)^{1/2}+\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}y_{t}^{4}(0)\right)^{1/2}\right]t^{1/2}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-2}~~~~\text{(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})^{2}(t\vee\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\int_{0}^{\eta_{T+1}^{-1}}x^{-1/2}\mathrm{d}x~~~~\text{($\eta_{t}$ is decreasing)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\eta_{T+1}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{1/2}\eta_{T}^{-1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

(99)

Hence by (
C.3
), (
C.3
), (
C.3
) and (
C.3
), we proved that the negative part of
\Esym[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]
is of order
o(T)+O(T^{1/2}\eta_{T}^{-1/2})=o(T)
by Assumption
3
. Hence the result is proved.
∎

The following lemma upper bounds the variability of the inverse probability for the last subject
T
.
Its proof follows a similar line of reasoning as that of Lemma
C.4
.
We recommend that readers consult the proof of Lemma
C.4
first.
For simplicity, some intermediate steps are omitted in the proof of Lemma
C.22
.

Lemma C.22
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, there holds:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\right],~\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{1-\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\right]\right\}=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{-5/24}R_{T}^{1/6}\rparen\kern 5.0pt,

Proof.

We first bound the variance of
\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)
and
\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)
. For
\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)
, by Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and (
C.2.1
) in the proof of Lemma
C.3
, we have

\displaystyle\Varsym\lparen\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)\rparen

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\operatorname{Var}\left(\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)\cdot(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{2}\right)~~~~(\text{variance of sum of MDS})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle)^{4}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1-p_{t}}{p_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}y_{t}^{4}(1)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{p_{t}}\right]+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle^{4}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1-p_{t}}{p_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{9/8}\rparen+T^{5/4}\max_{t\in[T]}R_{t}a_{t}^{1/4}(0)~~~~(\text{by \eqref{lem5.2_eq}, Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}, Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{5/4}\cdot\max_{t\in[T]}R_{t}(\eta_{t}T)^{1/4}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{11/8}\cdot\max_{t\in[T]}R_{t}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{11/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence there exists constant
C>0
such that
\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(1))\leq CT^{11/8}R_{T}^{1/2}
. Similarly, we can prove that
\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(0))\leq CT^{11/8}R_{T}^{1/2}
. Suppose
0<\delta<1/4
is a fixed constant. Then we consider the following three cases:

(1)

A_{T}^{*}(0)\leq(1-\delta)A_{T}^{*}(1)
.

For
k\in\{0,1\}
, denote events
\mathcal{G}_{k}=\left\{|\widetilde{A}_{T}(k)-A_{T}^{*}(k)|<\frac{\delta}{2}(A_{T}^{*}(1)\vee A_{T}^{*}(0))\right\}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{k}=\Big\{|\widetilde{A}_{T}(k)-A_{T}^{*}(k)|

<\frac{1}{2}(A_{T}^{*}(1)\vee A_{T}^{*}(0))\Big\}
.
By Lemma
4.9
and Lemma
C.21
, it is easy to see that
A_{T}^{*}(1)=\Theta(T)
and
A_{T}^{*}(0)=\Theta(T)
.
Hence by Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{1}^{c}\right)\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{-2}T^{-2}\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(1))\lesssim\delta^{-2}T^{-2}T^{11/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\lesssim\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{c}\right)\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{-2}T^{-2}\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(0))\lesssim\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}^{c})\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-2}\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(1))\lesssim T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Pr}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{c})\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-2}\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(0))\lesssim T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}\kern 5.0pt.

(100)

By Assumption
1
, under event
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)}{\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)}\leq\frac{{A}_{T}^{*}(0)+\frac{1}{2}{A}_{T}^{*}(0)}{{A}_{T}^{*}(1)-\frac{1}{2}{A}_{T}^{*}(1)}\leq\frac{3c_{1}}{c_{0}}\kern 5.0pt,

which implies that
1/\tilde{p}\leq 2+3^{1/2}b_{1}(2b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(c_{1}/c_{0})^{1/2}
by Lemma
4.14
. Moreover we have
1/\breve{p}\leq 2+b_{1}(2b_{2}/b_{3})^{1/2}(c_{1}/c_{0})^{1/2}
by Lemma
4.14
and Assumption
1
. Under event
\mathcal{G}_{1}
(
\mathcal{G}_{1}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}
) and
\mathcal{G}_{0}
(
\mathcal{G}_{0}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}
), we have
\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)\geq\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)
. Note that, by definition
\tilde{p}
and
\breve{p}
should satisfy the following first-order equations:

\displaystyle-\frac{\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)}{\tilde{p}^{2}}+\frac{\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)}{(1-\tilde{p})^{2}}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(\tilde{p})=

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle-\frac{A_{T}^{*}(1)}{\breve{p}^{2}}+\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)}{(1-\breve{p})^{2}}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(\breve{p})=

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Lemma
C.1
, we can show that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\frac{A_{T}^{*}(1)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)}{A_{T}^{*}(1)}\right|+\left|\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)}{A_{T}^{*}(0)}\right|\kern 5.0pt,

which indicates that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\frac{A_{T}^{*}(1)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)}{A_{T}^{*}(1)}\right)^{5/4}+\left(\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)}{A_{T}^{*}(0)}\right)^{5/4}\kern 5.0pt.

(101)

Hence by Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
, (
(1)
), (
101
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[\mathcal{G}_{1}\cap\mathcal{G}_{0}\right]\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[\mathcal{G}_{1}^{c}\cup\mathcal{G}_{0}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[\mathcal{G}_{1}\cap\mathcal{G}_{0}\right]\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[(\mathcal{G}_{1}^{c}\cup\mathcal{G}_{0}^{c})\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}\right]\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}^{c}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{A_{T}^{*}(1)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(1)}{A_{T}^{*}(1)}\right)^{5/4}+\left(\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)-\widetilde{A}_{T}(0)}{A_{T}^{*}(0)}\right)^{5/4}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\mathbf{1}\left[(\mathcal{G}_{1}^{c}\cup\mathcal{G}_{0}^{c})\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}\cap\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}\right]\right]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right)^{5/4}\mathbf{1}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}^{c}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-5/4}\operatorname{Var}^{5/8}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(1))+T^{-5/4}\operatorname{Var}^{5/8}(\widetilde{A}_{T}(0))+\operatorname{Pr}\left(\mathcal{G}_{1}^{c}\cup\mathcal{G}_{0}^{c}\right)+\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}^{4}}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}^{4}}\right|\right]^{5/16}\operatorname{Pr}^{11/16}\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{1}^{c}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{c}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-25/64}R_{T}^{5/16}+\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}+(\eta_{T}T)^{5/16}\cdot(T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2})^{11/16}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-25/64}R_{T}^{5/16}+\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}+T^{-35/128}R_{T}^{-9/32}\kern 5.0pt.

(102)

(2)

A_{T}(1)\leq(1-\delta)A_{T}(0)
.

By similar method as in case (1), we can prove that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\right]\lesssim T^{-25/64}R_{T}^{5/16}+\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}+T^{-35/128}R_{T}^{-9/32}\kern 5.0pt.

(103)

(3)

A_{T}(0)\geq(1-\delta)A_{T}(1)
and
A_{T}(1)\geq(1-\delta)A_{T}(0)
.

By similar proof as in Lemma
C.4
and in case (1), we can prove that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{\breve{p}}\right|^{5/4}\right]\lesssim\delta+\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}+T^{-35/128}R_{T}^{-9/32}\kern 5.0pt.

(104)

By choosing
\delta=T^{-5/24}R_{T}^{1/6}
, we can verify that

\displaystyle T^{-25/64}R_{T}^{5/16}=T^{-5/24}R_{T}^{1/6}\cdot(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-35/192}\cdot R_{T}^{-7/12}=o(\delta)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\delta^{-2}T^{-5/8}R_{T}^{1/2}=\delta\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle T^{-35/128}R_{T}^{-9/32}=T^{-5/24}R_{T}^{1/6}\cdot T^{-25/384}R_{T}^{-43/96}=o(\delta)\kern 5.0pt.

Hence the result is proved by (
(1)
), (
103
) and (
104
).

∎

The following lemma is proved by a similar argument as in Lemma
C.1
.
It characterizes the difference in the inverse probabilities when different step sizes are used.

Lemma C.23
.

Let
A,B,\eta\geq\tilde{\eta}
be positive constants.
Suppose
p,\tilde{p}
satisfy

\displaystyle-\frac{A}{p^{2}}+\frac{B}{(1-p)^{2}}+\eta^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(p)

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle-\frac{A}{\tilde{p}^{2}}+\frac{B}{(1-\tilde{p})^{2}}+\tilde{\eta}^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(\tilde{p})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt.

(1)

If
A\geq B
, then under Condition
1
we can attain the following upper bound:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6b_{1}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)^{5}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}\left(\frac{p}{1-p}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{p}}{1-\tilde{p}}\right)^{4}\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

If
A\leq B
, then under Condition
1
we can attain the following upper bound:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p}-\frac{1}{\tilde{p}}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6b_{1}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)^{5}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{B}\left(\frac{1-p}{p}\right)\left(\frac{1-\tilde{p}}{\tilde{p}}\right)^{4}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We only prove the first part.
Since
A\geq B
, we have
p,\tilde{p}\geq 1/2
.
For
u=\phi^{-1}(p)\geq 0
and
\tilde{u}=\phi^{-1}(\tilde{p})\geq 0
, it is easy to see that
u
and
\tilde{u}
satisfy

\displaystyle A\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}+B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}+\eta^{-1}(u+3u^{2})=

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle A\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}+B\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}+\tilde{\eta}^{-1}(\tilde{u}+3\tilde{u}^{2})=

\displaystyle 0\kern 5.0pt.

(105)

By subtracting the first equation in (
C.3
) from the second equation, we have

\displaystyle A\left[\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]+B\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle+\eta^{-1}(u-\tilde{u})(1+3u+3\tilde{u})+(\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1})(\tilde{u}+3\tilde{u}^{2})=0\kern 5.0pt.

(106)

The convexity assumption in Condition
1
implies that
A\left[\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]
,
B\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]
and
\eta^{-1}(u-\tilde{u})(1+3u+3\tilde{u})
always have the same sign. Hence by Lemma
B.16
, we have

\displaystyle\left|A\left[\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]+B\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right]+\eta^{-1}(u-\tilde{u})(1+3u+3\tilde{u})\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle A\left|\left(\frac{1}{\phi(u)}\right)^{\prime}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u})}\right)^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{b_{3}}{2}\frac{A}{(1+\tilde{u})(1+u)(1+\tilde{u}\land u)}\cdot|u-\tilde{u}|\kern 5.0pt.

(107)

Then by (
C.3
), (
C.3
) and Lemma
B.16
, we have

\displaystyle|u-\tilde{u}|\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{b_{3}}{2}\frac{A}{(1+\tilde{u})(1+u)(1+\tilde{u}\land u)}\right)^{-1}|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|(\tilde{u}+3\tilde{u}^{2})\quad(\text{by \eqref{coro5.2_eq2} and \eqref{coro5.2_eq3}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{b_{3}}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})^{2}(\tilde{u}+3\tilde{u}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6}{b_{3}}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}(1+u)(1+\tilde{u})^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6}{b_{3}}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}\left[\frac{2}{b_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-1/2}\right)+1\right]\left[\frac{2}{b_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}-\frac{1}{1-1/2}\right)+1\right]^{4}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem_u}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)^{5}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}\left(\frac{p}{1-p}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{p}}{1-\tilde{p}}\right)^{4}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we can prove by Lemma
B.16
that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{1-p}-\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}}\right|\leq b_{1}|u-\tilde{u}|\leq\frac{6b_{1}}{b_{3}}\left(1+\frac{2}{b_{3}}\right)^{5}\frac{|\eta^{-1}-\tilde{\eta}^{-1}|}{A}\left(\frac{p}{1-p}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{p}}{1-\tilde{p}}\right)^{4}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

With the help of Lemma
C.21
, Lemma
C.22
and Lemma
C.23
, now we can derive the exact form for the asymptotic variance of
\hat{\tau}
in the following theorem.

Theorem
5.1
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, the asymptotic variance of the adaptive AIPW estimator under
Sigmoid-FTRL
is the oracle variance:

T\cdot\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen=2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)+o\lparen 1\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

By Lemma
3.3
, we have

\displaystyle T\cdot\operatorname{Var}(\hat{\tau})=

\displaystyle T\cdot\textrm{V}^{*}+\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]\kern 5.0pt.

(108)

By Lemma
4.9
and Lemma
C.21
, we have shown that
\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}[\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{pred}}]=o(1)
. Hence it suffices to prove that
\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]=o(1)
.
We introduce the following notations:

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{t}(p)=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{p}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-p}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widehat{F}_{t}(p)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{f}_{s}(p)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t}}\Psi(p)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widetilde{F}_{t}(p)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{f}_{s}(p)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t-1}}\Psi(p)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widehat{h}_{t}(u)=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\cdot\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\phi(u)}+\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}\cdot\left(y_{t}(0)-\langle x_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(0)\rangle\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-\phi(u)}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widehat{H}_{t}(u)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{h}_{s}(u)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t}}\psi(u)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{t}(u)=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{h}_{s}(u)+\frac{1}{\eta_{t-1}}\psi(u)\kern 5.0pt.

Let
\tilde{p}_{t}
,
u_{t}
,
\tilde{u}_{t}
be the minimizer of function
\widetilde{F}_{t}
,
\widehat{H}_{t}
and
\widetilde{H}_{t}
, respectively.
By similar proof as in Lemma
4.9
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(p_{t})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{f}_{t}(p_{t})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{f}_{t}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{\eta_{T+1}}\Psi(p^{*})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{f}_{t}(p_{t})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{F}_{t}(p_{t})-\widehat{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})\right)+\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p_{T+1})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{\eta_{T+1}}\Psi(p^{*})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t})-\widehat{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})\right)+\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p_{T+1})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\frac{1}{\eta_{T}}\Psi(p^{*})+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t})-\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(\tilde{p}_{t+1})\right]-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})-\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(\tilde{p}_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})-\widehat{F}_{t+1}({p}_{t+1})\right]+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p_{T+1})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(1)-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})-\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(\tilde{p}_{t+1})\right]-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)\mathrm{E}\left[\Psi(p_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p_{T+1})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(\tilde{p})\right]+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{F}_{T+1}(\tilde{p})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(\breve{p})\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{F}_{T+1}(\breve{p})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p^{*})\right]~~~~(\text{Proposition \ref{prop:prob-regret-bound}})

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle o(1)-S_{1}-S_{2}+S_{3}+S_{4}+S_{5}\kern 5.0pt.

(109)

For
S_{1}
, by the definition of
\widetilde{F}
and
\widetilde{H}
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(p_{t+1})-\widetilde{F}_{t+1}(\tilde{p}_{t+1})\right)\right]\quad(\text{optimality of $\tilde{p}_{t+1}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(u_{t+1})-\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\langle\nabla\widetilde{H}_{t+1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1}),u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1}\rangle+\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]\quad(\text{optimality of $\tilde{u}_{t+1}$})\kern 5.0pt.

For any
t\in[T]
, if
\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\geq\widehat{A}_{t}(0)
, then
u_{t+1},\tilde{u}_{t+1}\geq 0
.
Since
\eta_{t+1}\leq\eta_{t}
, it is easy to show that
\tilde{u}_{t+1}\geq u_{t+1}
and
\tilde{p}_{t+1}\geq p_{t+1}
by similar method as in Lemma
4.14
.
Hence for
f_{1}(u)=1/\phi(u)
and
f_{2}(u)=1/(1-\phi(u))
, by Lemma
B.16
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{f_{1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})=

\displaystyle f_{1}(u_{t+1})-f_{1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})-f_{1}^{\prime}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\phi(u_{t+1})}-\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u}_{t+1})}-\left(\frac{1}{\phi(\tilde{u}_{t+1})}\right)^{\prime}(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{2}\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{f_{2}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})=

\displaystyle f_{2}(u_{t+1})-f_{2}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})-f_{2}^{\prime}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\phi(u_{t+1})}-\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u}_{t+1})}-\left(\frac{1}{1-\phi(\tilde{u}_{t+1})}\right)^{\prime}(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b_{2}\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})=

\displaystyle\psi(u_{t+1})-\psi(\tilde{u}_{t+1})-\psi^{\prime}(\tilde{u}_{t+1})(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}u_{t+1}^{2}+u_{t+1}^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{u}_{t+1}^{2}-\tilde{u}_{t+1}^{3}-(\tilde{u}_{t+1}+3\tilde{u}_{t+1}^{2})(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(u_{t+1}-\tilde{u}_{t+1})^{2}(1+2u_{t+1}+4\tilde{u}_{t+1})\kern 5.0pt.

On the other side, by the definition of
p_{t+1}
and
\tilde{p}_{t+1}
, there holds:

\displaystyle-\frac{\widehat{A}_{t}(1)}{\tilde{p}_{t+1}^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{A}_{t}(0)}{(1-\tilde{p}_{t+1})^{2}}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(\tilde{p}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle-\frac{\widehat{A}_{t}(1)}{p_{t+1}^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{A}_{t}(0)}{(1-p_{t+1})^{2}}+\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\Psi^{\prime}(p_{t+1})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt.

Then by Lemma
B.16
, Lemma
C.1
and Lemma
C.23
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\cdot\mathcal{B}_{f_{1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})+\widehat{A}_{t}(0)\cdot\mathcal{B}_{f_{2}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})+\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{B}_{\psi}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}+\widehat{A}_{t}(0)\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}+\eta_{t}^{-1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1})^{2}(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1}+u_{t+1})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}+\eta_{t}^{-1}(\tilde{u}_{t+1}-u_{t+1})^{2}(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})\quad(\text{since $\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\geq\widehat{A}_{t}(0)$ and $\tilde{u}_{t+1}\geq u_{t+1}$})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\cdot\frac{\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}}{(1+u_{t+1})(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}\right)^{2}(1+\tilde{u}_{t+1})\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem_u}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\cdot\left(\frac{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{p_{t+1}}{1-p_{t+1}}\right)^{-1}\cdot\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}}{\widehat{A}_{t}(1)}\left(\frac{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)\left(\frac{p_{t+1}}{1-p_{t+1}}\right)^{4}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{coro5.2}})

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\frac{\eta_{t}\widehat{A}_{t}(1)-\eta_{t+1}\widehat{A}_{t}(1)}{\eta_{t}\widehat{A}_{t}(1)}+\frac{p_{t+1}}{1-p_{t+1}}\frac{\eta_{t}\widehat{A}_{t}(0)-\eta_{t+1}\widehat{A}_{t}(0)}{\eta_{t}\widehat{A}_{t}(0)}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.3}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})\left(\frac{p_{t+1}}{1-p_{t+1}}\right)^{3}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}}{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)^{3}\quad(\text{since $\tilde{p}_{t+1}\geq p_{t+1}$})\kern 5.0pt.

If
\widehat{A}_{t}(1)\leq\widehat{A}_{t}(0)
, by symmetry we can similarly prove that

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\lesssim(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})\left(\frac{1-p_{t+1}}{p_{t+1}}\right)^{3}+\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}}{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)^{3}\kern 5.0pt.

Since
\eta_{t}\geq\eta_{t+1}
, by Corollary
4.15
, Corollary
B.17
and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}\right)^{3}+\left(\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}\right)^{3}\right]+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}}{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}}\right)^{2}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{1-\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)^{3}+\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{t+1}}\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})\eta_{t+1}^{3/4}T^{3/4}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}^{-1}\left(\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}}{\eta_{t+1}^{-1}}\right)^{2}\eta_{t}^{3/4}T^{3/4}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-3/4}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{t}^{-1/4}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})\eta_{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-3/4}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-3/4}\right]~~~~(\text{since $\eta_{t}^{-1/4}\leq\eta_{t+1}^{-1/4}$})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2T^{-1/4}\int_{\eta_{1}^{-1}}^{\eta_{T+1}^{-1}}x^{-3/4}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\eta_{T}^{-1/4}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt,

which implies that

\displaystyle S_{1}=\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathcal{B}_{\widetilde{H}_{t+1}}(u_{t+1}|\tilde{u}_{t+1})\right]=o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

(110)

For
p_{t+1}\geq 1/2
, by Lemma
B.16
, we have

\displaystyle\phi^{-1}(p_{t+1})=u_{t+1}-0\leq\frac{2}{b_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}-\frac{1}{1-1/2}\right)\lesssim\frac{1}{1-p_{t+1}}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we can prove that for
p_{t+1}\leq 1/2
, we have
-\phi^{-1}(p_{t+1})\lesssim\frac{1}{p_{t+1}}
. Hence by Corollary
4.15
and Corollary
B.17
, we have

\displaystyle S_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)\mathrm{E}\left[\Psi(p_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}(\phi^{-1}(p_{t+1}))^{2}+|\phi^{-1}(p_{t+1})|^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1}\right)\eta_{t+1}^{3/4}T^{3/4}~~~~(\text{Corollary \ref{corollary:p-moment} and Corollary \ref{coro4.7}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-\eta_{t}^{-1})(\eta_{t+1}^{-1})^{-3/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\int_{\eta_{1}^{-1}}^{\eta_{T+1}^{-1}}x^{-3/4}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\eta_{T}^{-1/4}

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle 0~~~~(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:maximum-radius}})\kern 5.0pt.

(111)

Let

. Then by Lemma

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{F}_{T+1}(\tilde{p})-\widehat{F}_{T+1}(p_{T+1})\right]\quad(\text{optimality of $p_{T+1}$})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\widehat{H}_{T+1}(\tilde{u})-\widehat{H}_{T+1}(u_{T+1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\langle\nabla\widehat{H}_{T+1}(u_{T+1}),\tilde{u}-u_{T+1}\rangle+\mathcal{B}_{\widehat{H}_{T+1}}(\tilde{u}|u_{T+1})\right]

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\left\{\left\langle-\nabla\widehat{H}_{T+1}(\tilde{u}),u_{t}-z\right\rangle-\frac{1}{2\eta_{T}}(z-\tilde{u})^{2}\left(1+|\tilde{u}|\right)\right\}\right]~~~~(\text{Lemma \ref{lemma:breg-lb}})

\displaystyle T^{-1}\eta_{T}\mathrm{E}\left[(\nabla\widehat{H}_{T+1}(\tilde{u}))^{2}\right]\kern 5.0pt.

By definition,

, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|A_{T}^{*}(1)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)|}{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)}+\frac{|A_{T}^{*}(0)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)-\Delta|}{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)}\quad(\text{Lemma \ref{lem5.3}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}\cdot o(T)+T^{-1}\cdot o(T)\quad(\text{Assumption \ref{assumption:moments}, Lemma \ref{coro4.3} and Lemma \ref{lem7.2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

\mathcal{E}_{1}\leq\mathcal{E}_{0}
and
A_{T}^{*}(1)\leq A_{T}^{*}(0)
.

We can prove that
\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|=o(1)
by the same method as in case (1).

(3)

\mathcal{E}_{1}\geq\mathcal{E}_{0}
and
A_{T}^{*}(1)\leq A_{T}^{*}(0)
.

In such case,
\breve{p}
and
p^{*}
satisfies:

\displaystyle-\frac{A_{T}^{*}(1)}{\breve{p}^{2}}+\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)}{(1-\breve{p})^{2}}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi(\breve{p})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle-\frac{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)}{(p^{*})^{2}}+\frac{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)+\Delta}{(1-p^{*})^{2}}+\eta_{T}^{-1}\Psi(p^{*})

\displaystyle=0\kern 5.0pt,

where
\Delta\triangleq-\eta_{T}^{-1}(1-p^{*})^{2}\Psi(p^{*})=o(T)
. Then by Lemma
B.11
and similar proof as in Lemma
C.21
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq A_{T}^{*}(0)-A_{T}^{*}(1)\leq

\displaystyle T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)+|A_{T}^{*}(1)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)|+|A_{T}^{*}(0)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|A_{T}^{*}(1)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)|+|A_{T}^{*}(0)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(T)\kern 5.0pt.

Then by Lemma
C.2
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-2\right|=\left|\frac{\breve{p}-(1/2)}{\breve{p}\cdot(1/2)}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\breve{p}-(1/2)}{(1-\breve{p})\cdot(1/2)}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{1-\breve{p}}-2\right|\leq\frac{A_{T}^{*}(0)-A_{T}^{*}(1)}{2A_{T}^{*}(1)}=o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly we can prove that
T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)=o(T)
. Then by Assumption
1
and Lemma
C.2
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p^{*}}-2\right|\leq\frac{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(1)-T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)-\Delta}{T\cdot\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)+\Delta}=o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|\leq\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-2\right|+\left|\frac{1}{p^{*}}-2\right|=o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

(4)

\mathcal{E}_{1}\leq\mathcal{E}_{0}
and
A_{T}^{*}(1)\geq A_{T}^{*}(0)
.

We can prove that
\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|=o(1)
by the same method as in case (3).

By the discussions in these four cases, we proved that
\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|=o(1)
. Then by Corollary
B.17
and (
C.2.1
), we have

\displaystyle|S_{5,2}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}\right]\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(y_{t}(1)-\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}^{*}(1)\rangle\right)^{2}+\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{t}(1)-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}(1)\rangle^{2}\right]\right)\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\breve{p}}-\frac{1}{p^{*}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}\cdot T\cdot o(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(1)\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we can show that
S_{5,3}=o(1)
.
Hence
S_{5}=o(1)
by (
C.3
), which indicates that
\frac{1}{T}\Esym[\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{prob}}_{T}]=o(1)-S_{1}-S_{2}+S_{3}+S_{4}+S_{5}=o(1)
by (
C.3
), (
110
), (
C.3
) and (
115
). Since
T\cdot\textrm{V}^{*}=2(1+\rho)\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)
, the result is proved.
∎

The explicit form of the asymptotic variance in Theorem
5.1
establishes the equivalence between Assumption
4
and the non-superefficiency condition, which is stated in the Corollary
5.2
.

Corollary
5.2

(Non-Superefficiency)
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
and Condition
1
,
\liminf_{T\to\infty}T\cdot\Varsym\lparen\hat{\tau}\rparen>0
.

Proof.

It is easy to see that
-1\leq\rho\leq 1
by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Since
c_{0}\leq\mathcal{E}(0),\mathcal{E}(1)\leq c_{1}
by Assumption
1
, the result is proved by Assumption
4
and Theorem
5.1
.
∎

C.4
Variance Estimator

Recall that the variance estimator is proposed as:

\displaystyle\widehat{A}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}Y_{t}^{2}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s\neq t}Q_{t,s}Y_{t}Y_{s}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1,Z_{s}=1]}{p_{s}p_{t}}\quad\text{and}\quad

\displaystyle\widehat{A}(0)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}Y_{t}^{2}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0]}{1-p_{t}}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s\neq t}Q_{t,s}Y_{t}Y_{s}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=0,Z_{s}=0]}{(1-p_{s})(1-p_{t})}\kern 5.0pt.

In this section, we establish the consistency of the proposed variance estimator and characterize its convergence rate.
The following lemma provides an upper bound for the deterministic terms involved in controlling the variance of the estimator.

Lemma C.24
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
, for
k\in\{0,1\}
, there holds:

(1)

\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}^{2}y_{t}^{4}(k)\leq c_{1}^{4}T
.

(2)

\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(k)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(k)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}\leq c_{1}^{4}c_{2}R_{T}^{2}
.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
k=1
.

(1)

We have
\boldsymbol{Q}=\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right)
by definition. Since
\boldsymbol{Q}\preceq\boldsymbol{I}_{T}
, we have
Q_{t,t}\leq\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|_{2}\leq 1
for any
t\in[T]
. Hence by Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}^{2}y_{t}^{4}(1)\leq c_{1}^{4}T\kern 5.0pt.

(2)

Denote
\boldsymbol{H}=\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}}=\operatorname{diag}\left\{\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{1}}\right)^{1/2},\ldots,\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{T}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\boldsymbol{H}\operatorname{diag}\left\{\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{1}}\right)^{1/2},\ldots,\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{T}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\kern 5.0pt.

It is easy to see that
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}}
is positive definite.
Denote
\boldsymbol{Y}=(y_{1}^{2}(1),\ldots,y_{T}^{2}(1))^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}
.
For any
1\leq i\neq j\leq T
, it is easy to see that
H_{ij}=-Q_{ij}
. Hence we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}H_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1},t_{2}\leq T}H_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{H}\circ\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}})\boldsymbol{Y}\kern 5.0pt.

The
t
-th diagonal element of
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}}
is bounded by:

\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{t,t}=\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t}}\right)H_{t,t}\leq\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{2}T^{-1}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t}}\right)R_{t}^{2}\leq c_{2}R_{T}^{2}T^{-1}\kern 5.0pt.

Then by Theorem 5.3.4 in
15
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{H}\circ\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}}\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{H}\|_{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\widetilde{H}_{t,t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\right\|_{2}\cdot c_{2}R_{T}^{2}T^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{T}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}\boldsymbol{X}_{T}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}\cdot c_{2}R_{T}^{2}T^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{2}R_{T}^{2}T^{-1}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Assumption
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Y}^{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}}(\boldsymbol{H}\circ\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}})\boldsymbol{Y}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{H}\circ\widetilde{\boldsymbol{H}}\|_{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}y_{t}^{4}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}^{4}c_{2}R_{T}^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

We now bound the variance of the estimated squared OLS in the following theorem:

Theorem
5.6
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
, for each treatment
k\in\{0,1\}
, we have that the estimated squared OLS residuals satisfy

\Esym[\widehat{A}(k)]=A(k)\quad\text{and}\quad\Varsym\lparen\widehat{A}(k)^{2}\rparen=\mathcal{O}\lparen\{T^{-5/12}R^{2/3}\}^{2}\rparen\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We only prove the result for
k=1
.
We first verify the unbiasedness of
\widehat{A}(1)
.
By law of iterated expectation, we have

\displaystyle\Esym[\widehat{A}(1)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}+\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\right]+\frac{2}{T}\sum_{1\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[\mathrm{E}\left[Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\frac{2}{T}\sum_{1\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq T}\mathrm{E}\left[Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)+\frac{2}{T}\sum_{1\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\boldsymbol{y}(k)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\intercal$}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\intercal$}}}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{y}(k)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A(1)\kern 5.0pt.

Now we turn to bounding the variance of
\widehat{A}(1)
.
By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and AM-GM inequality, we have:

\displaystyle\Varsym\lparen\widehat{A}(1)\rparen

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\operatorname{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}+\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\operatorname{Var}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}y_{t}^{2}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}\right)+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\operatorname{Var}\left(\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}y_{t_{1}}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)\cdot\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}Q_{t,t}^{2}y_{t}^{4}(1)\operatorname{Var}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\operatorname{Var}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}Q_{t_{1},t_{3}}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}(1)y_{t_{3}}(1)\operatorname{Cov}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}},\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}\right)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}\kern 5.0pt.

By similar method as in the proof of Lemma
B.18
and Lemma
C.17
, we can show that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq t\leq T}\operatorname{Var}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t}=1]}{p_{t}}-1\right)=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{1/8}\rparen\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}\operatorname{Var}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\lparen T^{9/32}\rparen\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}},\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(2+b_{1}(b_{2}/6)^{1/4}\eta_{t_{1}}^{1/4}\mathrm{E}^{1/4}\widehat{A}_{t_{1}}(0)\right)^{81/64}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{9/128}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{9/128}\kern 5.0pt.

Then by Lemma
C.24
, AM-GM inequality and Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle|S_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}\cdot T^{1/8}=T^{-7/8},

\displaystyle|S_{2}|\lesssim

\displaystyle R_{T}^{2}T^{-2}\cdot T^{9/32}=T^{-1}\cdot(TR_{T}^{-4})^{-23/32}\cdot R_{T}^{-7/8}=o(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle|S_{3}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}|Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}||Q_{t_{1},t_{3}}|y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)|y_{t_{2}}(1)||y_{t_{3}}(1)|\left|\operatorname{Cov}\left(\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{2}}=1]}{p_{t_{2}}},\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{1}}=1]}{p_{t_{1}}}\frac{\mathbf{1}[Z_{t_{3}}=1]}{p_{t_{3}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}|Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}||Q_{t_{1},t_{3}}|y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)|y_{t_{2}}(1)||y_{t_{3}}(1)|(\eta_{t_{1}}T)^{81/256}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{9/128}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{9/128}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}|Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}||Q_{t_{1},t_{3}}|y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)|y_{t_{2}}(1)||y_{t_{3}}(1)|(\eta_{T}T)^{81/256}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{81/128}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{9/128}\left(\frac{R_{t_{1}}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{9/128}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}\neq t_{3}<t_{1}\leq T}|Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}||Q_{t_{1},t_{3}}|y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)|y_{t_{2}}(1)||y_{t_{3}}(1)|(\eta_{T}T)^{81/256}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{63/128}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{9/128}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{3}}}\right)^{9/128}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}(\eta_{T}T)^{81/256}\sum_{1\leq t_{2}<t_{1}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{63/128}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{9/64}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}(\eta_{T}T)^{81/256}\sum_{1\leq t_{1}\neq t_{2}\leq T}Q_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{2}y_{t_{1}}^{2}(1)y_{t_{2}}^{2}(1)\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{1}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{R_{T}}{R_{t_{2}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}(\eta_{T}T)^{81/256}R_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}R_{T}^{2}(TR_{T}^{-4})^{81/512}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}R_{T}^{2}(TR_{T}^{-4})^{1/6}\kern 5.0pt.

Hence by Lemma
B.5
, we have

\displaystyle\Varsym\lparen\widehat{A}(1)\rparen\lesssim

\displaystyle S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-7/8}+T^{-1}+T^{-1}R_{T}^{2}(TR_{T}^{-4})^{1/6}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}R_{T}^{2}(TR_{T}^{-4})^{1/6}\cdot\left(T^{-1/24}R_{T}^{-4/3}+T^{-1/6}R_{T}^{-4/3}+1\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{-1}R_{T}^{2}(TR_{T}^{-4})^{1/6}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-5/6}R_{T}^{4/3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T^{-5/12}R^{2/3})^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

∎

The following corollary is implied by Theorem
5.6
, which implies the consistency of the variance estimator.

Corollary
5.7
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
3
and Condition
1
,

T\cdot\widehat{\textrm{VB}}-T\cdot\textrm{VB}=\mathcal{O}_{p}\lparen T^{-5/12}R^{2/3}\rparen
.

Proof.

Recall that
T\cdot\textrm{VB}=4\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)
and
T\cdot\widehat{\textrm{VB}}=4\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(1)\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(0)
. By the definition of
\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(k)
and Theorem
5.6
, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{\mathcal{E}}^{2}(k)-\mathcal{E}^{2}(k)|\leq|\widehat{A}(k)-\mathcal{E}^{2}(k)|=|\widehat{A}(k)-A(k)|=\mathcal{O}_{p}(T^{-5/12}R^{2/3})\kern 5.0pt.

Since
\mathcal{E}(k)=\Theta(1)
by Assumption
1
, it is easy to show that
|\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(k)-\mathcal{E}(k)|=\mathcal{O}_{p}(T^{-5/24}R^{1/3})
. Then by Assumption
1
, we can directly show that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(1)\widehat{\mathcal{E}}(0)-\mathcal{E}(1)\mathcal{E}(0)=\mathcal{O}_{p}(T^{-5/12}R^{2/3})\kern 5.0pt,

which implies that
T\cdot\widehat{\textrm{VB}}-T\cdot\textrm{VB}=\mathcal{O}_{p}(T^{-5/12}R^{2/3})
.
∎

C.5
Wald-type Confidence Intervals

Under the central limit theorem (Theorem
C.20
) and the consistency of the variance estimator (Corollary
5.7
), it is easy to prove the following lemma regarding the asymptotic coverage of the Wald-type confidence intervals.

Corollary
5.8
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
and Condition
1
, the Wald-type intervals cover at the nominal level:
\liminf_{T\to\infty}\Pr\lparen\tau\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{\alpha}\rparen\geq 1-\alpha
.
